<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰουδαίων (the Jews), critical Ἰουδαίου (a Jew)" data-ref="John 3:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 3:25</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰουδαίων (the Jews), critical Ἰουδαίου (a Jew)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then there arose a dispute between some of John's disciples and <mark class="chg">the Jews</mark> about purification.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then there arose a dispute between some of John's disciples and <mark class="chg">a Jew</mark> about purification.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ὁ Χριστός (the Christ) before σωτήρ; the CT has “the Saviour of the world” only" data-ref="John 4:42"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 4:42</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ὁ Χριστός (the Christ) before σωτήρ; the CT has “the Saviour of the world” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and they said to the woman, Now we believe, not because of your saying, for we have heard for ourselves and know that this is indeed the <mark class="chg">Christ, the</mark> Savior of the world.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and they said to the woman, Now we believe, not because of your saying, for we have heard for ourselves and know that this is indeed the Savior of the world.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ ἐζήτουν αὐτὸν ἀποκτεῖναι (and sought to kill him); the CT has “persecution” only" data-ref="John 5:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 5:16</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ ἐζήτουν αὐτὸν ἀποκτεῖναι (and sought to kill him); the CT has “persecution” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For this reason the Jews persecuted Jesus, <mark class="chg">and sought to kill Him,</mark> because He had done these things on the Sabbath.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For this reason the Jews persecuted Jesus, because He had done these things on the Sabbath.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT διέδωκε τοῖς μαθηταῖς, οἱ δὲ μαθηταὶ τοῖς ἀνακειμένοις (gave to the disciples, and the disciples to those reclining); the CT collapses the twofold distribution" data-ref="John 6:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 6:11</span><span class="srnote">RT διέδωκε τοῖς μαθηταῖς, οἱ δὲ μαθηταὶ τοῖς ἀνακειμένοις (gave to the disciples, and the disciples to those reclining); the CT collapses the twofold distribution</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And Jesus took the loaves, and when He had given thanks He distributed them to <mark class="chg">the disciples, and the disciples to</mark> those sitting down; and likewise of the fish, as much as they wanted.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And Jesus took the loaves, and when He had given thanks He distributed them to those sitting down; and likewise of the fish, as much as they wanted.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὁ πιστεύων εἰς ἐμέ (believes in me); the CT has “the one who believes” with no object" data-ref="John 6:47"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 6:47</span><span class="srnote">RT ὁ πιστεύων εἰς ἐμέ (believes in me); the CT has “the one who believes” with no object</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Most assuredly, I say to you, he who believes <mark class="chg">in Me</mark> has everlasting life.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Most assuredly, I say to you, he who believes has everlasting life.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads οἱ πατέρες ὑμῶν τὸ μάννα (your fathers ate the manna); the CT renders it “what your fathers ate and died”, dropping the manna the verse names" data-ref="John 6:58"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 6:58</span><span class="srnote">RT reads οἱ πατέρες ὑμῶν τὸ μάννα (your fathers ate the manna); the CT renders it “what your fathers ate and died”, dropping the manna the verse names</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This is the bread which came down from heaven—not as your fathers ate <mark class="chg">the manna</mark> and died. He who eats this bread will live forever.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This is the bread which came down from heaven—not as your fathers ate <mark class="chg">that bread</mark> and died. He who eats this bread will live forever.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads σὺ εἶ ὁ Χριστὸς ὁ υἱὸς τοῦ Θεοῦ τοῦ ζῶντος (the Christ, the Son of the living God); the CT’s “the Holy One of God” follows the critical ὁ ἅγιος τοῦ θεοῦ" data-ref="John 6:69"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 6:69</span><span class="srnote">RT reads σὺ εἶ ὁ Χριστὸς ὁ υἱὸς τοῦ Θεοῦ τοῦ ζῶντος (the Christ, the Son of the living God); the CT’s “the Holy One of God” follows the critical ὁ ἅγιος τοῦ θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Also we have come to believe and know that You are the <mark class="chg">Christ, the Son</mark> of <mark class="chg">the living</mark> God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Also we have come to believe and know that You are the <mark class="chg">Holy One</mark> of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ ἐλθὼν νυκτὸς πρὸς αὐτόν (who came to him by night); the CT’s “before” follows the critical πρότερον and drops the night detail" data-ref="John 7:50"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 7:50</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ ἐλθὼν νυκτὸς πρὸς αὐτόν (who came to him by night); the CT’s “before” follows the critical πρότερον and drops the night detail</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nicodemus, who <mark class="chg">came</mark> to Him <mark class="chg">by night,</mark> and who was one of them, said to them,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nicodemus, who <mark class="chg">had gone</mark> to Him <mark class="chg">before,</mark> and who was one of them, said to them,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἃ ἑωράκατε παρὰ τῷ πατρὶ ὑμῶν (what you have seen with your father); the CT’s “heard” follows the critical ἠκούσατε" data-ref="John 8:38"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 8:38</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἃ ἑωράκατε παρὰ τῷ πατρὶ ὑμῶν (what you have seen with your father); the CT’s “heard” follows the critical ἠκούσατε</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I speak what I have seen with My Father, and you do what you have <mark class="chg">seen with</mark> your father.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I speak what I have seen with My Father, and you do what you have <mark class="chg">heard from</mark> your father.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds διελθὼν διὰ μέσου αὐτῶν καὶ παρῆγεν οὕτως (passing through their midst he went on), which the CT omits" data-ref="John 8:59"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 8:59</span><span class="srnote">RT adds διελθὼν διὰ μέσου αὐτῶν καὶ παρῆγεν οὕτως (passing through their midst he went on), which the CT omits</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then they took up stones to throw at Him; but Jesus hid Himself and went out of the <mark class="chg">temple, going through the midst of them, and so passed by.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then they took up stones to throw at Him; but Jesus hid Himself and went out of the <mark class="chg">temple.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἐμὲ δεῖ ἐργάζεσθαι (I must work); the CT’s “we” follows the critical ἡμᾶς" data-ref="John 9:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 9:4</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἐμὲ δεῖ ἐργάζεσθαι (I must work); the CT’s “we” follows the critical ἡμᾶς</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">I</mark> must work the works of Him who sent Me while it is day; the night is coming when no one can work.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">We</mark> must work the works of Him who sent Me while it is day; the night is coming when no one can work.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὅτι τυφλὸς ἦν (that he was blind); the CT’s “beggar” follows the critical προσαίτης" data-ref="John 9:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 9:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὅτι τυφλὸς ἦν (that he was blind); the CT’s “beggar” follows the critical προσαίτης</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore the neighbors and those who previously had seen that he was <mark class="chg">blind</mark> said, Is not this he who sat and begged?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore the neighbors and those who previously had seen that he was <mark class="chg">a beggar</mark> said, Is not this he who sat and begged?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ Θεοῦ (the Son of God); the CT’s “the Son of Man” follows the critical τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ ἀνθρώπου" data-ref="John 9:35"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 9:35</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ Θεοῦ (the Son of God); the CT’s “the Son of Man” follows the critical τὸν υἱὸν τοῦ ἀνθρώπου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Jesus heard that they had cast him out; and when He had found him, He said to him, Do you believe in the Son of <mark class="chg">God?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Jesus heard that they had cast him out; and when He had found him, He said to him, Do you believe in the Son of <mark class="chg">Man?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes with καθὼς εἶπον ὑμῖν (as I said to you), which the CT omits" data-ref="John 10:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 10:26</span><span class="srnote">RT closes with καθὼς εἶπον ὑμῖν (as I said to you), which the CT omits</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But you do not believe, because you are not of My <mark class="chg">sheep, as I said to you.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But you do not believe, because you are not of My <mark class="chg">sheep.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads συμφέρει ἡμῖν (it is expedient for us); the CT’s “to you” follows the critical ὑμῖν" data-ref="John 11:50"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 11:50</span><span class="srnote">RT reads συμφέρει ἡμῖν (it is expedient for us); the CT’s “to you” follows the critical ὑμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">nor do you consider that it is expedient for <mark class="chg">us</mark> that one man should die for the people, and not that the whole nation should perish.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">nor do you consider that it is expedient for <mark class="chg">you</mark> that one man should die for the people, and not that the whole nation should perish.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Ἰούδας Σίμωνος Ἰσκαριώτης (Judas, Simon’s son); the CT drops the patronymic here" data-ref="John 12:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 12:4</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Ἰούδας Σίμωνος Ἰσκαριώτης (Judas, Simon's son); the CT drops the patronymic here</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then one of His disciples, Judas Iscariot, <mark class="chg">Simon's son,</mark> who would betray Him, said</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then one of His disciples, Judas Iscariot, who would betray Him, said</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τετήρηκεν αὐτό (she has kept it); the CT’s “let her keep it” follows the critical τηρήσῃ" data-ref="John 12:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 12:7</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τετήρηκεν αὐτό (she has kept it); the CT’s “let her keep it” follows the critical τηρήσῃ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let her <mark class="chg">alone;</mark> she <mark class="chg">has kept</mark> it for the day of My burial.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let her <mark class="chg">alone, that</mark> she <mark class="chg">may keep</mark> it for the day of My burial.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads μὴ πιστεύσῃ (does not believe); the CT’s “does not keep” follows the critical φυλάξῃ" data-ref="John 12:47"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 12:47</span><span class="srnote">RT reads μὴ πιστεύσῃ (does not believe); the CT’s “does not keep” follows the critical φυλάξῃ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And if anyone hears My words and does not <mark class="chg">believe,</mark> I do not judge him; for I did not come to judge the world but to save the world.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And if anyone hears My words and does not <mark class="chg">keep them,</mark> I do not judge him; for I did not come to judge the world but to save the world.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ τρώγων μετ᾿ ἐμοῦ τὸν ἄρτον (he who eats bread with me); the CT’s “he who eats my bread” follows the critical μου τὸν ἄρτον" data-ref="John 13:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 13:18</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ τρώγων μετ᾿ ἐμοῦ τὸν ἄρτον (he who eats bread with me); the CT’s “he who eats my bread” follows the critical μου τὸν ἄρτον</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I do not speak concerning all of you. I know whom I have chosen; but that the Scripture may be fulfilled, He who eats bread <mark class="chg">with Me</mark> has lifted up his heel against Me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I do not speak concerning all of you. I know whom I have chosen; but that the Scripture may be fulfilled, He who eats <mark class="chg">My</mark> bread has lifted up his heel against Me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τηρήσατε (aorist imperative: keep!), where the critical text has τηρήσετε (future); the CT renders the promise form “will keep” instead of the command" data-ref="John 14:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 14:15</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τηρήσατε (aorist imperative: keep!), where the critical text has τηρήσετε (future); the CT renders the promise form “will keep” instead of the command</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If you love Me, keep My commandments.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If you love Me, <mark class="chg">you will</mark> keep My commandments.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT αἰτήσεσθε is future indicative (’you will ask’), not the imperative “ask”" data-ref="John 15:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 15:7</span><span class="srnote">RT αἰτήσεσθε is future indicative ('you will ask'), not the imperative “ask”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If you abide in Me, and My words abide in you, <mark class="chg">you will</mark> ask whatever you desire, and it shall be done for you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If you abide in Me, and My words abide in you, ask whatever you desire, and it shall be done for you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ends the saying with ὅτι ἐγὼ ὑπάγω πρὸς τὸν πατέρα (because I go to the Father), dropped by the CT though 16:17 goes on to quote it" data-ref="John 16:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 16:16</span><span class="srnote">RT ends the saying with ὅτι ἐγὼ ὑπάγω πρὸς τὸν πατέρα (because I go to the Father), dropped by the CT though 16:17 goes on to quote it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">A little while, and you will not see Me; and again a little while, and you will see <mark class="chg">Me, because I go to the Father.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">A little while, and you will not see Me; and again a little while, and you will see <mark class="chg">Me.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Ὅτε ἤμην μετ᾿ αὐτῶν ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ (with them in the world); the CT drops ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ" data-ref="John 17:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 17:12</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Ὅτε ἤμην μετ᾿ αὐτῶν ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ (with them in the world); the CT drops ἐν τῷ κόσμῳ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">While I was with <mark class="chg">them in the world,</mark> I kept them in Your name. Those whom You gave Me I have kept; and none of them is lost except the son of perdition, that the Scripture might be fulfilled.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">While I was with <mark class="chg">them,</mark> I kept them in Your name. Those whom You gave Me I have kept; and none of them is lost except the son of perdition, that the Scripture might be fulfilled.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has the vocative Θωμᾶ (“Thomas”); the CT leaves the name out" data-ref="John 20:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 20:29</span><span class="srnote">RT has the vocative Θωμᾶ (“Thomas”); the CT leaves the name out</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Jesus said to him, <mark class="chg">Thomas, because</mark> you have seen Me, you have believed. Blessed are those who have not seen and yet have believed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Jesus said to him, <mark class="chg">Because</mark> you have seen Me, you have believed. Blessed are those who have not seen and yet have believed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical" data-ref="John 21:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 21:15</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So when they had eaten breakfast, Jesus said to Simon Peter, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">Jonah,</mark> do you love Me more than these? He said to Him, Yes, Lord; You know that I love You. He said to him, Feed My lambs.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So when they had eaten breakfast, Jesus said to Simon Peter, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">John,</mark> do you love Me more than these? He said to Him, Yes, Lord; You know that I love You. He said to him, Feed My lambs.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical" data-ref="John 21:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 21:16</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He said to him again a second time, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">Jonah,</mark> do you love Me? He said to Him, Yes, Lord; You know that I love You. He said to him, Tend My sheep.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He said to him again a second time, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">John,</mark> do you love Me? He said to Him, Yes, Lord; You know that I love You. He said to him, Tend My sheep.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical" data-ref="John 21:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 21:17</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); the CT’s John is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He said to him the third time, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">Jonah,</mark> do you love Me? Peter was grieved because He said to him the third time, Do you love Me? And he said to Him, Lord, You know all things; You know that I love You. Jesus said to him, Feed My sheep.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He said to him the third time, Simon, son of <mark class="chg">John,</mark> do you love Me? Peter was grieved because He said to him the third time, Do you love Me? And he said to Him, Lord, You know all things; You know that I love You. Jesus said to him, Feed My sheep.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the book with Ἀμήν (Amen), absent from the CT" data-ref="John 21:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 21:25</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the book with Ἀμήν (Amen), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And there are also many other things that Jesus did, which if they were written one by one, I suppose that even the world itself could not contain the books that would be written. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And there are also many other things that Jesus did, which if they were written one by one, I suppose that even the world itself could not contain the books that would be written.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Acts</span><span class="bkcount">87</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has τῇ προσευχῇ καὶ τῇ δεήσει (prayer and supplication); the CT keeps only “prayer”" data-ref="Acts 1:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 1:14</span><span class="srnote">RT has τῇ προσευχῇ καὶ τῇ δεήσει (prayer and supplication); the CT keeps only “prayer”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">These all continued with one accord in <mark class="chg">prayer and supplication,</mark> with the women and Mary the mother of Jesus, and with His brothers.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">These all continued with one accord in <mark class="chg">prayer,</mark> with the women and Mary the mother of Jesus, and with His brothers.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἐν μέσῳ τῶν μαθητῶν (among the disciples), not τῶν ἀδελφῶν (“brethren”)" data-ref="Acts 1:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 1:15</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἐν μέσῳ τῶν μαθητῶν (among the disciples), not τῶν ἀδελφῶν (“brethren”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And in those days Peter stood up in the midst of the <mark class="chg">disciples</mark> (altogether the number of names was about a hundred and twenty), and said,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And in those days Peter stood up in the midst of the <mark class="chg">brethren</mark> (altogether the number of names was about a hundred and twenty), and said,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has τὸ κατὰ σάρκα ἀναστήσειν τὸν Χριστόν (raise up the Christ according to the flesh); the CT keeps only “one man”" data-ref="Acts 2:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 2:30</span><span class="srnote">RT has τὸ κατὰ σάρκα ἀναστήσειν τὸν Χριστόν (raise up the Christ according to the flesh); the CT keeps only “one man”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore, being a prophet, and knowing that God had sworn with an oath to him that of the fruit of his <mark class="chg">body, according to the flesh,</mark> He would <mark class="chg">raise up the Christ to sit</mark> on his throne,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore, being a prophet, and knowing that God had sworn with an oath to him that of the fruit of his <mark class="chg">body</mark> He would <mark class="chg">set one</mark> on his throne,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has τῇ ἐκκλησίᾳ (to the church); the critical text reads ἐπὶ τὸ αὐτό and the CT drops the object" data-ref="Acts 2:47"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 2:47</span><span class="srnote">RT has τῇ ἐκκλησίᾳ (to the church); the critical text reads ἐπὶ τὸ αὐτό and the CT drops the object</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">praising God and having favor with all the people. And the Lord added to <mark class="chg">the church</mark> daily those who were being saved.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">praising God and having favor with all the people. And the Lord added to <mark class="chg">their number</mark> daily those who were being saved.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τὸν προκεκηρυγμένον (preached beforehand), not τὸν προκεχειρισμένον (“appointed”, appointed)" data-ref="Acts 3:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 3:20</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τὸν προκεκηρυγμένον (preached beforehand), not τὸν προκεχειρισμένον (“appointed”, appointed)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and that He may send Jesus Christ, who was <mark class="chg">preached to you before,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and that He may send Jesus Christ, who was <mark class="chg">appointed for you,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Μωσῆς … πρὸς τοὺς πατέρας εἶπεν (Moses said to the fathers); the CT omits the audience" data-ref="Acts 3:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 3:22</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Μωσῆς … πρὸς τοὺς πατέρας εἶπεν (Moses said to the fathers); the CT omits the audience</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For Moses truly <mark class="chg">said to the fathers,</mark> The Lord your God will raise up for you a prophet like me from your brethren. Him you shall hear in all things, whatever He says to you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For Moses truly <mark class="chg">said,</mark> The Lord your God will raise up for you a prophet like me from your brethren. Him you shall hear in all things, whatever He says to you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT names the servant as Ἰησοῦν (“your servant Jesus”); the CT has “your servant” only" data-ref="Acts 3:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 3:26</span><span class="srnote">RT names the servant as Ἰησοῦν (“your servant Jesus”); the CT has “your servant” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To you first, God, having raised up His <mark class="chg">Servant Jesus,</mark> sent Him to bless you, in turning away every one of you from your iniquities.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To you first, God, having raised up His <mark class="chg">Servant,</mark> sent Him to bless you, in turning away every one of you from your iniquities.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads πρεσβύτεροι τοῦ Ἰσραήλ (elders of Israel); the CT drops “of Israel”" data-ref="Acts 4:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 4:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads πρεσβύτεροι τοῦ Ἰσραήλ (elders of Israel); the CT drops “of Israel”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then Peter, filled with the Holy Spirit, said to them, Rulers of the people and <mark class="chg">elders of Israel,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then Peter, filled with the Holy Spirit, said to them, Rulers of the people and <mark class="chg">elders,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has σύ ὁ Θεός (You are God) after Δέσποτα; the CT keeps only “O Lord”" data-ref="Acts 4:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 4:24</span><span class="srnote">RT has σύ ὁ Θεός (You are God) after Δέσποτα; the CT keeps only “O Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So when they heard that, they raised their voice to God with one accord and said, Lord, <mark class="chg">You are God,</mark> who made heaven and earth and the sea, and all that is in them,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So when they heard that, they raised their voice to God with one accord and said, Lord, who made heaven and earth and the sea, and all that is in them,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT lacks πατρὸς ἡμῶν (“our fathers”) and πνεύματος ἁγίου (“by the Holy Spirit”), both read in the CT from the CT" data-ref="Acts 4:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 4:25</span><span class="srnote">RT lacks πατρὸς ἡμῶν (“our fathers”) and πνεύματος ἁγίου (“by the Holy Spirit”), both read in the CT from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">who by the mouth of Your servant <mark class="chg">David</mark> said, Why did the nations rage, and the people plot vain things?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">who by the mouth of Your servant <mark class="chg">our father David, through the Holy Spirit,</mark> said, Why did the nations rage, and the people plot vain things?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰωσῆς, critical Ἰωσήφ; the CT’s Joseph is the critical name" data-ref="Acts 4:36"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 4:36</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰωσῆς, critical Ἰωσήφ; the CT’s Joseph is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And <mark class="chg">Joses,</mark> who was also named Barnabas by the apostles (which is translated Son of Encouragement), a Levite of the country of Cyprus,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And <mark class="chg">Joseph,</mark> who was also named Barnabas by the apostles (which is translated Son of Encouragement), a Levite of the country of Cyprus,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has three groups: ὅ τε ἱερεὺς καὶ ὁ στρατηγὸς τοῦ ἱεροῦ καὶ οἱ ἀρχιερεῖς; the CT drops “chief priests”" data-ref="Acts 5:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 5:24</span><span class="srnote">RT has three groups: ὅ τε ἱερεὺς καὶ ὁ στρατηγὸς τοῦ ἱεροῦ καὶ οἱ ἀρχιερεῖς; the CT drops “chief priests”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now when the <mark class="chg">priests,</mark> the captain of the <mark class="chg">temple, and the chief priests</mark> heard these words, they were perplexed about them, wondering what this would come to.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now when the <mark class="chg">priests and</mark> the captain of the <mark class="chg">temple</mark> heard these words, they were perplexed about them, wondering what this would come to.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT λαὸν ἱκανόν (a considerable number of people); the CT has “the people” only" data-ref="Acts 5:37"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 5:37</span><span class="srnote">RT λαὸν ἱκανόν (a considerable number of people); the CT has “the people” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">After this man, Judas of Galilee rose up in the days of the census, and drew away <mark class="chg">many</mark> people after him. He also perished, and all who obeyed him were dispersed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">After this man, Judas of Galilee rose up in the days of the census, and drew away <mark class="chg">some of the</mark> people after him. He also perished, and all who obeyed him were dispersed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πίστεως (faith), not χάριτος (“grace”) which the CT renders" data-ref="Acts 6:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 6:8</span><span class="srnote">RT πίστεως (faith), not χάριτος (“grace”) which the CT renders</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And Stephen, full of <mark class="chg">faith</mark> and power, did great wonders and signs among the people.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And Stephen, full of <mark class="chg">grace</mark> and power, did great wonders and signs among the people.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ῥήματα βλάσφημα λαλῶν (speaking blasphemous words); the CT has “speaking against” only" data-ref="Acts 6:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 6:13</span><span class="srnote">RT ῥήματα βλάσφημα λαλῶν (speaking blasphemous words); the CT has “speaking against” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">They also set up false witnesses who said, This man does not cease to speak <mark class="chg">blasphemous words</mark> against this holy place and the law;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">They also set up false witnesses who said, This man does not cease to speak against this holy place and the law;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἐμμὼρ τοῦ Συχέμ (Hamor the father of Shechem), not the critical ἐν Συχέμ" data-ref="Acts 7:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:16</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἐμμὼρ τοῦ Συχέμ (Hamor the father of Shechem), not the critical ἐν Συχέμ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And they were carried back to Shechem and laid in the tomb that Abraham bought for a sum of money from the sons of <mark class="chg">Hamor, the father of</mark> Shechem.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And they were carried back to Shechem and laid in the tomb that Abraham bought for a sum of money from the sons of <mark class="chg">Hamor in</mark> Shechem.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὤμοσεν (swore) with ἐπαγγελία; the CT’s “promised” follows the critical ὡμολόγησεν" data-ref="Acts 7:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:17</span><span class="srnote">RT ὤμοσεν (swore) with ἐπαγγελία; the CT’s “promised” follows the critical ὡμολόγησεν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But when the time of the promise drew near which God had <mark class="chg">sworn</mark> to Abraham, the people grew and multiplied in Egypt,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But when the time of the promise drew near which God had <mark class="chg">promised</mark> to Abraham, the people grew and multiplied in Egypt,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἄγγελος Κυρίου (Angel of the Lord); the CT has “an angel” only" data-ref="Acts 7:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:30</span><span class="srnote">RT ἄγγελος Κυρίου (Angel of the Lord); the CT has “an angel” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when forty years had passed, an Angel <mark class="chg">of the Lord</mark> appeared to him in a flame of fire in a bush, in the wilderness of Mount Sinai.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when forty years had passed, an Angel appeared to him in a flame of fire in a bush, in the wilderness of Mount Sinai.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT repeats ὁ Θεός before each patriarch (ὁ Θεὸς Ἀβραάμ, ὁ Θεὸς Ἰσαάκ, ὁ Θεὸς Ἰακώβ); the CT collapses the three" data-ref="Acts 7:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:32</span><span class="srnote">RT repeats ὁ Θεός before each patriarch (ὁ Θεὸς Ἀβραάμ, ὁ Θεὸς Ἰσαάκ, ὁ Θεὸς Ἰακώβ); the CT collapses the three</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">saying, I am the God of your fathers, the God of Abraham, <mark class="chg">the God</mark> of Isaac, and <mark class="chg">the God</mark> of Jacob. And Moses trembled and dared not look.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">saying, I am the God of your fathers, the God of Abraham, of Isaac, and of Jacob. And Moses trembled and dared not look.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has Κύριος ὁ Θεὸς ὑμῶν (“the Lord your God”) and closes with αὐτοῦ ἀκούσεσθε (“you shall hear him”), both absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 7:37"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:37</span><span class="srnote">RT has Κύριος ὁ Θεὸς ὑμῶν (“the Lord your God”) and closes with αὐτοῦ ἀκούσεσθε (“you shall hear him”), both absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This is that Moses who said to the children of Israel, <mark class="chg">The Lord your</mark> God will raise up for you a prophet like me from your brethren. <mark class="chg">Him you shall hear.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This is that Moses who said to the children of Israel, God will raise up for you a prophet like me from your brethren.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν χειροποιήτοις ναοῖς (temples made with hands); the CT generalizes to “a place”" data-ref="Acts 7:48"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 7:48</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν χειροποιήτοις ναοῖς (temples made with hands); the CT generalizes to “a place”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">However, the Most High does not dwell in <mark class="chg">temples</mark> made with hands, as the prophet says:</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">However, the Most High does not dwell in <mark class="chg">houses</mark> made with hands, as the prophet says:</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦ Θεοῦ; the CT’s “to the Lord” follows the critical τοῦ Κυρίου" data-ref="Acts 8:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 8:22</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦ Θεοῦ; the CT’s “to the Lord” follows the critical τοῦ Κυρίου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Repent therefore of this your wickedness, and pray to <mark class="chg">God,</mark> if perhaps the thought of your heart may be forgiven you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Repent therefore of this your wickedness, and pray to <mark class="chg">the Lord,</mark> if perhaps the thought of your heart may be forgiven you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT" data-ref="Acts 8:37"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 8:37</span><span class="srnote">printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Then Philip said, If you believe with all your heart, you may. And he answered and said, I believe that Jesus Christ is the Son of God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has Ὁ δὲ Κύριος εἶπεν and the clause σκληρόν σοι πρὸς κέντρα λακτίζειν (hard for you to kick against the goads), both absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 9:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 9:5</span><span class="srnote">RT has Ὁ δὲ Κύριος εἶπεν and the clause σκληρόν σοι πρὸς κέντρα λακτίζειν (hard for you to kick against the goads), both absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And he said, Who are You, Lord? Then <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> said, I am Jesus, whom you are persecuting. <mark class="chg">It is hard for you to kick against the goads.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And he said, Who are You, Lord? Then <mark class="chg">He</mark> said, I am Jesus, whom you are persecuting.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds the trembling question and ’the Lord said to him’" data-ref="Acts 9:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 9:6</span><span class="srnote">RT adds the trembling question and 'the Lord said to him'</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">So he, trembling and astonished, said, Lord, what do You want me to do? Then the Lord said to him,</mark> Arise and go into the city, and you will be told what you must do.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Arise and go into the city, and you will be told what you must do.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Χριστόν, critical Ἰησοῦν; the CT’s Jesus is the critical name" data-ref="Acts 9:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 9:20</span><span class="srnote">RT Χριστόν, critical Ἰησοῦν; the CT’s Jesus is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Immediately he preached <mark class="chg">the Christ</mark> in the synagogues, that He is the Son of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Immediately he preached <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> in the synagogues, that He is the Son of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads αἱ ἐκκλησίαι … εἶχον … ἐπληθύνοντο (plural churches and verbs); the CT follows the critical singular" data-ref="Acts 9:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 9:31</span><span class="srnote">RT reads αἱ ἐκκλησίαι … εἶχον … ἐπληθύνοντο (plural churches and verbs); the CT follows the critical singular</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then the <mark class="chg">churches</mark> throughout all Judea, Galilee, and Samaria had peace and <mark class="chg">were</mark> edified. And walking in the fear of the Lord and in the comfort of the Holy Spirit, <mark class="chg">they were</mark> multiplied.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then the <mark class="chg">church</mark> throughout all Judea, Galilee, and Samaria had peace and <mark class="chg">was</mark> edified. And walking in the fear of the Lord and in the comfort of the Holy Spirit, <mark class="chg">it was</mark> multiplied.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds οὗτος λαλήσει σοι τί σε δεῖ ποιεῖν (he will tell you what you must do)" data-ref="Acts 10:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:6</span><span class="srnote">RT adds οὗτος λαλήσει σοι τί σε δεῖ ποιεῖν (he will tell you what you must do)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He is lodging with Simon, a tanner, whose house is by the sea. <mark class="chg">He will tell you what you must do.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He is lodging with Simon, a tanner, whose house is by the sea.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has four classes, including τὰ θηρία (wild beasts) alongside the four-footed animals; the CT has three" data-ref="Acts 10:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:12</span><span class="srnote">RT has four classes, including τὰ θηρία (wild beasts) alongside the four-footed animals; the CT has three</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">In it were all kinds of four-footed animals of the earth, <mark class="chg">wild beasts,</mark> creeping things, and birds of the air.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">In it were all kinds of four-footed animals of the earth, creeping things, and birds of the air.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἄνδρες τρεῖς (three men); the CT has “two men”" data-ref="Acts 10:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:19</span><span class="srnote">RT ἄνδρες τρεῖς (three men); the CT has “two men”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">While Peter thought about the vision, the Spirit said to him, "Behold, <mark class="chg">three</mark> men are seeking you."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">While Peter thought about the vision, the Spirit said to him, "Behold, <mark class="chg">two</mark> men are seeking you."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοὺς ἀπεσταλμένους ἀπὸ τοῦ Κορνηλίου πρὸς αὐτόν (sent from Cornelius); the CT has “those men” only" data-ref="Acts 10:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:21</span><span class="srnote">RT τοὺς ἀπεσταλμένους ἀπὸ τοῦ Κορνηλίου πρὸς αὐτόν (sent from Cornelius); the CT has “those men” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then Peter went down to the men <mark class="chg">whom Cornelius had sent to him,</mark> and said, "Behold, I am he whom you seek. Why have you come?"</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then Peter went down to the men and said, "Behold, I am he whom you seek. Why have you come?"</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἤμην νηστεύων (I was fasting); the CT omits “fasting”" data-ref="Acts 10:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:30</span><span class="srnote">RT ἤμην νηστεύων (I was fasting); the CT omits “fasting”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So Cornelius said, "Four days ago, until this hour, I was <mark class="chg">fasting and</mark> praying in my house at the ninth hour; and behold, a man stood before me in bright clothing,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So Cornelius said, "Four days ago, until this hour, I was praying in my house at the ninth hour; and behold, a man stood before me in bright clothing,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes with ὃς παραγενόμενος λαλήσει σοι (when he comes he will speak to you), absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 10:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:32</span><span class="srnote">RT closes with ὃς παραγενόμενος λαλήσει σοι (when he comes he will speak to you), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Send therefore to Joppa and call Simon here, whose surname is Peter. He is lodging in the house of Simon, a tanner, by the sea. <mark class="chg">When he comes, he will speak to you.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Send therefore to Joppa and call Simon here, whose surname is Peter. He is lodging in the house of Simon, a tanner, by the sea.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has τοῦ Θεοῦ twice; the CT’s “the Lord” follows the critical κυρίου" data-ref="Acts 10:33"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:33</span><span class="srnote">RT has τοῦ Θεοῦ twice; the CT’s “the Lord” follows the critical κυρίου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So I sent to you immediately, and you have done well to come. Now therefore, we are all present before God, to hear all the things commanded you by <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So I sent to you immediately, and you have done well to come. Now therefore, we are all present before God, to hear all the things commanded you by <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν τῷ ὀνόματι τοῦ Κυρίου (in the name of the Lord); the CT has “in the name of Jesus Christ” from the CT" data-ref="Acts 10:48"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 10:48</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν τῷ ὀνόματι τοῦ Κυρίου (in the name of the Lord); the CT has “in the name of Jesus Christ” from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So he commanded them to be baptized in the name of <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark> Then they asked him to stay a few days.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So he commanded them to be baptized in the name of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ.</mark> Then they asked him to stay a few days.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐπὶ Κλαυδίου Καίσαρος; the CT drops “Caesar”" data-ref="Acts 11:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 11:28</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐπὶ Κλαυδίου Καίσαρος; the CT drops “Caesar”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then one of them, named Agabus, stood up and showed by the Spirit that there was going to be a great famine throughout all the world, which also happened in the days of <mark class="chg">Claudius Caesar.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then one of them, named Agabus, stood up and showed by the Spirit that there was going to be a great famine throughout all the world, which also happened in the days of <mark class="chg">Claudius.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT κατεκληροδότησεν αὐτοῖς (he distributed to them) ends the verse; the CT carries the 450 years here and defers the clause to v.20" data-ref="Acts 13:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 13:19</span><span class="srnote">RT κατεκληροδότησεν αὐτοῖς (he distributed to them) ends the verse; the CT carries the 450 years here and defers the clause to v.20</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when He had destroyed seven nations in the land of Canaan, He <mark class="chg">distributed</mark> their land <mark class="chg">to them by allotment.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when He had destroyed seven nations in the land of Canaan, He <mark class="chg">gave them</mark> their land <mark class="chg">as an inheritance — about four hundred and fifty years.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT numbers the 450 years with the giving of the judges (ὡς ἔτεσι τετρακοσίοις καὶ πεντήκοντα ἔδωκε κριτάς), not with the conquest as in the CT v.19" data-ref="Acts 13:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 13:20</span><span class="srnote">RT numbers the 450 years with the giving of the judges (ὡς ἔτεσι τετρακοσίοις καὶ πεντήκοντα ἔδωκε κριτάς), not with the conquest as in the CT v.19</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">After that He gave them judges <mark class="chg">for about four hundred and fifty years,</mark> until Samuel the prophet.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">After that He gave them judges until Samuel the prophet.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὑμῖν (to you); the CT’s “to us” follows the critical ἡμῖν" data-ref="Acts 13:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 13:26</span><span class="srnote">RT ὑμῖν (to you); the CT’s “to us” follows the critical ἡμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Men and brethren, sons of the family of Abraham, and those among you who fear God, to <mark class="chg">you</mark> the word of this salvation has been sent.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Men and brethren, sons of the family of Abraham, and those among you who fear God, to <mark class="chg">us</mark> the word of this salvation has been sent.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐκ τῆς συναγωγῆς τῶν Ἰουδαίων … παρεκάλουν τὰ ἔθνη; the CT has “they” … “the people”, losing both groups" data-ref="Acts 13:42"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 13:42</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐκ τῆς συναγωγῆς τῶν Ἰουδαίων … παρεκάλουν τὰ ἔθνη; the CT has “they” … “the people”, losing both groups</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So when <mark class="chg">the Jews</mark> went out of the synagogue, the <mark class="chg">Gentiles</mark> begged that these words might be preached to them the next Sabbath.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So when <mark class="chg">they</mark> went out of the synagogue, the <mark class="chg">people</mark> begged that these words might be preached to them the next Sabbath.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἥλλετο καὶ περιεπάτει (he leaped and walked); the CT keeps only “he rose and walked”" data-ref="Acts 14:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 14:10</span><span class="srnote">RT ἥλλετο καὶ περιεπάτει (he leaped and walked); the CT keeps only “he rose and walked”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He said with a loud voice, "Stand up straight on your feet." And he rose and <mark class="chg">leaped and</mark> walked.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He said with a loud voice, "Stand up straight on your feet." And he rose and walked.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡμῖν / ἡμῶν (to us, our hearts), not the critical ὑμῖν / ὑμῶν that the CT renders “you”" data-ref="Acts 14:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 14:17</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡμῖν / ἡμῶν (to us, our hearts), not the critical ὑμῖν / ὑμῶν that the CT renders “you”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nevertheless He did not leave Himself without witness, in that He did good, gave <mark class="chg">us</mark> rain from heaven and fruitful seasons, filling <mark class="chg">our</mark> hearts with food and gladness.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nevertheless He did not leave Himself without witness, in that He did good, gave <mark class="chg">you</mark> rain from heaven and fruitful seasons, filling <mark class="chg">your</mark> hearts with food and gladness.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has Χριστοῦ after Ἰησοῦ (“the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ”); the CT drops Christ" data-ref="Acts 15:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:11</span><span class="srnote">RT has Χριστοῦ after Ἰησοῦ (“the grace of our Lord Jesus Christ”); the CT drops Christ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But we believe that through the grace of the Lord Jesus <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> we shall be saved in the same manner as they.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But we believe that through the grace of the Lord Jesus we shall be saved in the same manner as they.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the verse λέγει Κύριος ὁ ποιῶν ταῦτα πάντα (says the Lord who does all these things), which the CT moved/lost" data-ref="Acts 15:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:17</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the verse λέγει Κύριος ὁ ποιῶν ταῦτα πάντα (says the Lord who does all these things), which the CT moved/lost</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So that the rest of mankind may seek the Lord, even all the Gentiles who are called by My <mark class="chg">name, says the Lord who does all these things.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So that the rest of mankind may seek the Lord, even all the Gentiles who are called by My <mark class="chg">name.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT γνωστὰ ἀπ᾽ αἰῶνός ἐστι τῷ Θεῷ πάντα τὰ ἔργα αὐτοῦ; the CT follows the critical γνωστὸν … τῷ κυρίῳ τὸ ἔργον" data-ref="Acts 15:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:18</span><span class="srnote">RT γνωστὰ ἀπ᾽ αἰῶνός ἐστι τῷ Θεῷ πάντα τὰ ἔργα αὐτοῦ; the CT follows the critical γνωστὸν … τῷ κυρίῳ τὸ ἔργον</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Known to God</mark> from <mark class="chg">eternity are all His works.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">That is, the Lord who makes these things known</mark> from <mark class="chg">of old.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT λέγοντες περιτέμνεσθαι καὶ τηρεῖν τὸν νόμον (saying you must be circumcised and keep the law) is absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 15:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:24</span><span class="srnote">RT λέγοντες περιτέμνεσθαι καὶ τηρεῖν τὸν νόμον (saying you must be circumcised and keep the law) is absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Since we have heard that some who went out from us without our instruction have troubled you with words, unsettling your <mark class="chg">minds, saying you must be circumcised and keep the law.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Since we have heard that some who went out from us without our instruction have troubled you with words, unsettling your <mark class="chg">minds.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πρὸς τοὺς ἀποστόλους (to the apostles); the CT follows the critical ἀποστείλαντας αὐτούς (“those who sent them”)" data-ref="Acts 15:33"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:33</span><span class="srnote">RT πρὸς τοὺς ἀποστόλους (to the apostles); the CT follows the critical ἀποστείλαντας αὐτούς (“those who sent them”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And after they had stayed there for a time, they were sent back with greetings from the brethren to <mark class="chg">the apostles.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And after they had stayed there for a time, they were sent back with greetings from the brethren to <mark class="chg">those who had sent them.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT" data-ref="Acts 15:34"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:34</span><span class="srnote">printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">However, it seemed good to Silas to remain there.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT χάριτι τοῦ Θεοῦ (grace of God), not τοῦ κυρίου (“the grace of the Lord”)" data-ref="Acts 15:40"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 15:40</span><span class="srnote">RT χάριτι τοῦ Θεοῦ (grace of God), not τοῦ κυρίου (“the grace of the Lord”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but Paul chose Silas and departed, being commended by the brethren to the grace of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but Paul chose Silas and departed, being commended by the brethren to the grace of <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὸ πνεῦμα without Ἰησοῦ; the CT adds “of Jesus” from the CT" data-ref="Acts 16:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 16:7</span><span class="srnote">RT τὸ πνεῦμα without Ἰησοῦ; the CT adds “of Jesus” from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">After they had come to Mysia, they tried to go into Bithynia, but the Spirit did not permit them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">After they had come to Mysia, they tried to go into Bithynia, but the Spirit <mark class="chg">of Jesus</mark> did not permit them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT κύριος (the Lord), not θεός (God)" data-ref="Acts 16:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 16:10</span><span class="srnote">RT κύριος (the Lord), not θεός (God)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now after he had seen the vision, immediately we sought to go to Macedonia, concluding that <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> had called us to preach the gospel to them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now after he had seen the vision, immediately we sought to go to Macedonia, concluding that <mark class="chg">God</mark> had called us to preach the gospel to them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐνομίζετο προσευχὴ εἶναι (where prayer was customarily made) and ἔξω τῆς πόλεως; the CT follows the critical ἐνομιζόμεν … πύλης (“outside the gate”, “supposing a place of prayer”)" data-ref="Acts 16:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 16:13</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐνομίζετο προσευχὴ εἶναι (where prayer was customarily made) and ἔξω τῆς πόλεως; the CT follows the critical ἐνομιζόμεν … πύλης (“outside the gate”, “supposing a place of prayer”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And on the Sabbath day we went <mark class="chg">out of</mark> the <mark class="chg">city</mark> to the riverside, <mark class="chg">where prayer</mark> was <mark class="chg">customarily made,</mark> and we sat down and spoke to the women who met there.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And on the Sabbath day we went <mark class="chg">outside</mark> the <mark class="chg">gate</mark> to the riverside, <mark class="chg">supposing there</mark> was <mark class="chg">a place of prayer,</mark> and we sat down and spoke to the women who met there.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡμῖν (to us), not ὑμῖν (“to you”)" data-ref="Acts 16:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 16:17</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡμῖν (to us), not ὑμῖν (“to you”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This woman followed Paul and us, and cried out, saying, These men are servants of the Most High God, who proclaim to <mark class="chg">us</mark> the way of salvation.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This woman followed Paul and us, and cried out, saying, These men are servants of the Most High God, who proclaim to <mark class="chg">you</mark> the way of salvation.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πίστευσον ἐπὶ τὸν κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; the CT omits Christ" data-ref="Acts 16:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 16:31</span><span class="srnote">RT πίστευσον ἐπὶ τὸν κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; the CT omits Christ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So they said, Believe on the Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> and you will be saved, you and your household.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So they said, Believe on the Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> and you will be saved, you and your household.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT οἱ ἀπειθοῦντες Ἰουδαῖοι (the disobedient Jews) — the CT has no equivalent for ἀπειθοῦντες" data-ref="Acts 17:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 17:5</span><span class="srnote">RT οἱ ἀπειθοῦντες Ἰουδαῖοι (the disobedient Jews) — the CT has no equivalent for ἀπειθοῦντες</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But the <mark class="chg">Jews who were not persuaded,</mark> becoming envious, took some of the evil men from the marketplace, and gathering a mob, set all the city in an uproar and attacked the house of Jason, and sought to bring them out to the people.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But the <mark class="chg">Jews,</mark> becoming envious, took some of the evil men from the marketplace, and gathering a mob, set all the city in an uproar and attacked the house of Jason, and sought to bring them out to the people.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὸν Κύριον (the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (God)" data-ref="Acts 17:27"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 17:27</span><span class="srnote">RT τὸν Κύριον (the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (God)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">so that they might grope for <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> and find Him, though He is not far from each one of us.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">so that they might grope for <mark class="chg">God</mark> and find Him, though He is not far from each one of us.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT συνείχετο τῷ πνεύματι (constrained in/by the Spirit); the CT follows the critical τῷ λόγῳ (“constrained by the word”)" data-ref="Acts 18:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 18:5</span><span class="srnote">RT συνείχετο τῷ πνεύματι (constrained in/by the Spirit); the CT follows the critical τῷ λόγῳ (“constrained by the word”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">When Silas and Timothy had come from Macedonia, Paul was <mark class="chg">compelled</mark> by the <mark class="chg">Spirit,</mark> testifying to the Jews that Jesus is the Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">When Silas and Timothy had come from Macedonia, Paul was <mark class="chg">constrained</mark> by the <mark class="chg">word of God,</mark> testifying to the Jews that Jesus is the Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has only Ἰούστου; the critical Τιτίου Ἰούστου behind the CT’s Titus Justus is absent" data-ref="Acts 18:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 18:7</span><span class="srnote">RT has only Ἰούστου; the critical Τιτίου Ἰούστου behind the CT’s Titus Justus is absent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And he departed from there and entered the house of a certain man named Justus, one who worshiped God, whose house was next door to the synagogue.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And he departed from there and entered the house of a certain man named <mark class="chg">Titius</mark> Justus, one who worshiped God, whose house was next door to the synagogue.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT οἱ Ἕλληνες (the Greeks) is present; the CT’s “all” drops the subject" data-ref="Acts 18:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 18:17</span><span class="srnote">RT οἱ Ἕλληνες (the Greeks) is present; the CT’s “all” drops the subject</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then all the <mark class="chg">Greeks</mark> seized Sosthenes, the ruler of the synagogue, and beat him before the judgment seat. But Gallio took no notice of these things.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then all the <mark class="chg">people</mark> seized Sosthenes, the ruler of the synagogue, and beat him before the judgment seat. But Gallio took no notice of these things.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT δεῖ με πάντως τὴν ἑορτὴν τὴν ἐρχομένην ποιῆσαι εἰς Ἱεροσόλυμα (I must by all means keep the coming feast in Jerusalem) is missing from the CT" data-ref="Acts 18:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 18:21</span><span class="srnote">RT δεῖ με πάντως τὴν ἑορτὴν τὴν ἐρχομένην ποιῆσαι εἰς Ἱεροσόλυμα (I must by all means keep the coming feast in Jerusalem) is missing from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but took leave of them, saying, I <mark class="chg">must by all means keep this coming feast in Jerusalem; but I</mark> will return <mark class="chg">again</mark> to you, God willing. And he sailed from Ephesus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but took leave of them, saying, I will return to you, God willing. And he sailed from Ephesus.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT κυρίου in both places, critical Ἰησοῦ; the CT’s Jesus is critical" data-ref="Acts 18:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 18:25</span><span class="srnote">RT κυρίου in both places, critical Ἰησοῦ; the CT’s Jesus is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This man had been instructed in the way of the Lord; and being fervent in spirit, he spoke and taught accurately the things concerning <mark class="chg">the Lord,</mark> though he knew only the baptism of John.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This man had been instructed in the way of the Lord; and being fervent in spirit, he spoke and taught accurately the things concerning <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> though he knew only the baptism of John.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT εἰς τὸν Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (on Christ Jesus); the CT has only Jesus" data-ref="Acts 19:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 19:4</span><span class="srnote">RT εἰς τὸν Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (on Christ Jesus); the CT has only Jesus</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then Paul said, John indeed baptized with a baptism of repentance, saying to the people that they should believe on Him who would come after him, that is, on <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> Jesus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then Paul said, John indeed baptized with a baptism of repentance, saying to the people that they should believe on Him who would come after him, that is, on Jesus.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὸν λόγον τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the word of the Lord Jesus); the CT omits Jesus" data-ref="Acts 19:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 19:10</span><span class="srnote">RT τὸν λόγον τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the word of the Lord Jesus); the CT omits Jesus</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And this continued for two years, so that all who dwelt in Asia heard the word of the <mark class="chg">Lord Jesus,</mark> both Jews and Greeks.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And this continued for two years, so that all who dwelt in Asia heard the word of the <mark class="chg">Lord,</mark> both Jews and Greeks.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τῆς μεγάλης θεᾶς Ἀρτέμιδος (of the great goddess Artemis); the CT omits “goddess”" data-ref="Acts 19:35"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 19:35</span><span class="srnote">RT τῆς μεγάλης θεᾶς Ἀρτέμιδος (of the great goddess Artemis); the CT omits “goddess”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when the city clerk had quieted the crowd, he said, Men of Ephesus, what man is there who does not know that the city of the Ephesians is temple guardian of the great <mark class="chg">goddess</mark> Artemis and of the image which fell down from Zeus?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when the city clerk had quieted the crowd, he said, Men of Ephesus, what man is there who does not know that the city of the Ephesians is temple guardian of the great Artemis and of the image which fell down from Zeus?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT θεὰν ὑμῶν (your goddess), not the critical θεὸν ἡμῶν (“our goddess”)" data-ref="Acts 19:37"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 19:37</span><span class="srnote">RT θεὰν ὑμῶν (your goddess), not the critical θεὸν ἡμῶν (“our goddess”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For you have brought these men here who are neither robbers of temples nor blasphemers of <mark class="chg">your</mark> goddess.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For you have brought these men here who are neither robbers of temples nor blasphemers of <mark class="chg">our</mark> goddess.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads only Σώπατρος Βεροιαῖος (Sopater of Berea); the critical Πύρρου behind the CT’s “of Pyrrhus” is absent" data-ref="Acts 20:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:4</span><span class="srnote">RT reads only Σώπατρος Βεροιαῖος (Sopater of Berea); the critical Πύρρου behind the CT’s “of Pyrrhus” is absent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And Sopater of <mark class="chg">Berea</mark> accompanied him; and of the Thessalonians, Aristarchus and Secundus; and Gaius of Derbe, and Timothy; and the Asians, Tychicus and Trophimus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And Sopater of <mark class="chg">Berea, the son of Pyrrhus,</mark> accompanied him; and of the Thessalonians, Aristarchus and Secundus; and Gaius of Derbe, and Timothy; and the Asians, Tychicus and Trophimus.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT συνηγμένων τῶν μαθητῶν (when the disciples had gathered), not the critical ἡμῶν (“we”)" data-ref="Acts 20:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:7</span><span class="srnote">RT συνηγμένων τῶν μαθητῶν (when the disciples had gathered), not the critical ἡμῶν (“we”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now on the first day of the week, when <mark class="chg">the disciples came together</mark> to break bread, Paul, ready to depart the next day, spoke to them and continued his message until midnight.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now on the first day of the week, when <mark class="chg">we had gathered</mark> to break bread, Paul, ready to depart the next day, spoke to them and continued his message until midnight.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ μείναντες ἐν Τρωγυλλίῳ (and having stayed at Trogyllium), absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 20:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:15</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ μείναντες ἐν Τρωγυλλίῳ (and having stayed at Trogyllium), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">We sailed from there, and the next day came opposite Chios. The following day we arrived at <mark class="chg">Samos and stayed at Trogyllium.</mark> The next day we came to Miletus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">We sailed from there, and the next day came opposite Chios. The following day we arrived at <mark class="chg">Samos.</mark> The next day we came to Miletus.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μετὰ πολλῶν δακρύων (with many tears); the CT has “tears” without “many”" data-ref="Acts 20:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:19</span><span class="srnote">RT μετὰ πολλῶν δακρύων (with many tears); the CT has “tears” without “many”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">serving the Lord with all humility, with <mark class="chg">many</mark> tears, and with trials which came upon me by the plots of the Jews</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">serving the Lord with all humility, with tears, and with trials which came upon me by the plots of the Jews</span></span></tr>
