<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τελειῶσαι τὸν δρόμον μου μετὰ χαρᾶς (finish my race with joy); the CT omits “with joy”" data-ref="Acts 20:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:24</span><span class="srnote">RT τελειῶσαι τὸν δρόμον μου μετὰ χαρᾶς (finish my race with joy); the CT omits “with joy”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But none of these things move me; nor do I count my life dear to myself, so that I may finish my race <mark class="chg">with joy,</mark> and the ministry which I received from the Lord Jesus, to testify to the gospel of the grace of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But none of these things move me; nor do I count my life dear to myself, so that I may finish my race and the ministry which I received from the Lord Jesus, to testify to the gospel of the grace of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT opens with Ἀδελφοί (“brethren”), a vocative the CT drops" data-ref="Acts 20:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:32</span><span class="srnote">RT opens with Ἀδελφοί (“brethren”), a vocative the CT drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So <mark class="chg">now, brethren,</mark> I commend you to God and to the word of His grace, which is able to build you up and give you an inheritance among all those who are sanctified.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So <mark class="chg">now</mark> I commend you to God and to the word of His grace, which is able to build you up and give you an inheritance among all those who are sanctified.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐδόξαζον τὸν Κύριον (glorified the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (God)" data-ref="Acts 21:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:20</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐδόξαζον τὸν Κύριον (glorified the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (God)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when they heard it, they glorified <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark> And they said to him, You see, brother, how many myriads of Jews there are who have believed, and they are all zealous for the law.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when they heard it, they glorified <mark class="chg">God.</mark> And they said to him, You see, brother, how many myriads of Jews there are who have believed, and they are all zealous for the law.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT δεῖ πλῆθος συνελθεῖν (the multitude must assemble) is missing from the CT" data-ref="Acts 21:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:22</span><span class="srnote">RT δεῖ πλῆθος συνελθεῖν (the multitude must assemble) is missing from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">What then? <mark class="chg">The assembly must certainly meet, for they</mark> will hear that you have come.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">What then? <mark class="chg">They</mark> will <mark class="chg">certainly</mark> hear that you have come.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μηδὲν τοιοῦτον τηρεῖν αὐτούς, εἰ μὴ (that they observe nothing of the kind except) is absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 21:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:25</span><span class="srnote">RT μηδὲν τοιοῦτον τηρεῖν αὐτούς, εἰ μὴ (that they observe nothing of the kind except) is absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But concerning the Gentiles who believe, we have written and decided that they should <mark class="chg">observe nothing of the kind, except that they</mark> keep themselves from things offered to idols, from blood, from things strangled, and from sexual immorality.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But concerning the Gentiles who believe, we have written and decided that they should keep themselves from things offered to idols, from blood, from things strangled, and from sexual immorality.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT καὶ ἔμφοβοι ἐγένοντο (and were afraid); the CT omits “they were afraid”" data-ref="Acts 22:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 22:9</span><span class="srnote">RT καὶ ἔμφοβοι ἐγένοντο (and were afraid); the CT omits “they were afraid”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And those who were with me indeed saw the <mark class="chg">light and were afraid,</mark> but they did not hear the voice of Him who spoke to me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And those who were with me indeed saw the <mark class="chg">light,</mark> but they did not hear the voice of Him who spoke to me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads μὴ θεομαχῶμεν (let us not fight against God); the CT reads open question “what shall we do”" data-ref="Acts 23:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:9</span><span class="srnote">RT reads μὴ θεομαχῶμεν (let us not fight against God); the CT follows that reading's open question “what shall we do”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then there arose a loud outcry. And the scribes of the Pharisees' party arose and protested, saying, We find no evil in this man; but if a spirit or an angel has spoken to him, <mark class="chg">let us not fight against God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then there arose a loud outcry. And the scribes of the Pharisees' party arose and protested, saying, We find no evil in this man; but if a spirit or an angel has spoken to him, <mark class="chg">what then?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has the vocative Παῦλε (Θάρσει Παῦλε); the CT drops the name" data-ref="Acts 23:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:11</span><span class="srnote">RT has the vocative Παῦλε (Θάρσει Παῦλε); the CT drops the name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But the following night the Lord stood by him and said, Be of good <mark class="chg">cheer, Paul;</mark> for as you have testified for Me in Jerusalem, so you must also bear witness at Rome.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But the following night the Lord stood by him and said, Be of good <mark class="chg">cheer;</mark> for as you have testified for Me in Jerusalem, so you must also bear witness at Rome.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the letter with Ἔρρωσο (Farewell), which the CT omits; the impersonal passive already carries ὑπὸ τῶν Ἰουδαίων" data-ref="Acts 23:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:30</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the letter with Ἔρρωσο (Farewell), which the CT omits; the impersonal passive already carries ὑπὸ τῶν Ἰουδαίων</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when it was told me that the Jews lay in wait for the man, I sent him to you immediately, and also commanded his accusers to state before you the charges against him. <mark class="chg">Farewell.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when it was told me that the Jews lay in wait for the man, I sent him to you immediately, and also commanded his accusers to state before you the charges against him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ κατὰ τὸν ἡμέτερον νόμον ἠθελήσαμεν κρίνειν (we wanted to judge him by our law), absent from the CT" data-ref="Acts 24:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:6</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ κατὰ τὸν ἡμέτερον νόμον ἠθελήσαμεν κρίνειν (we wanted to judge him by our law), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He also tried to profane the temple, and we seized <mark class="chg">him, and wanted to judge him according to our law.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He also tried to profane the temple, and we seized <mark class="chg">him.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="the CT has no verse 7 here; the RT does" data-ref="Acts 24:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:7</span><span class="srnote">the CT has no verse 7 here; the RT does</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="rest">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">But the commander Lysias came by and with great violence took him out of our hands.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT opens with κελεύσας τοὺς κατηγόρους αὐτοῦ ἔρχεσθαι ἐπὶ σέ; the CT lacks the clause" data-ref="Acts 24:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:8</span><span class="srnote">RT opens with κελεύσας τοὺς κατηγόρους αὐτοῦ ἔρχεσθαι ἐπὶ σέ; the CT lacks the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">commanding his accusers to come to you.</mark> By examining him yourself you may ascertain all these things of which we accuse him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">By examining him yourself you may ascertain all these things of which we accuse him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ὅπως λύσῃ αὐτόν (that he might release him), the motive behind the bribes" data-ref="Acts 24:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:26</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ὅπως λύσῃ αὐτόν (that he might release him), the motive behind the bribes</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Meanwhile he also hoped that money would be given him by <mark class="chg">Paul, that he might release him.</mark> Therefore he sent for him more often and conversed with him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Meanwhile he also hoped that money would be given him by <mark class="chg">Paul.</mark> Therefore he sent for him more often and conversed with him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has εἰς ἀπώλειαν (to destruction); the CT’s “what I endure” alone loses it" data-ref="Acts 25:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 25:16</span><span class="srnote">RT has εἰς ἀπώλειαν (to destruction); the CT’s “what I endure” alone loses it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To whom I answered, It is not the custom of the Romans to deliver up any man <mark class="chg">to destruction</mark> before the accused has met the accusers face to face, and has had opportunity to answer for himself concerning the charge.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To whom I answered, It is not the custom of the Romans to deliver up any man before the accused has met the accusers face to face, and has had opportunity to answer for himself concerning the charge.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT opens Καὶ ταῦτα εἰπόντος αὐτοῦ (when he had said these things), dropped in the CT" data-ref="Acts 26:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 26:30</span><span class="srnote">RT opens Καὶ ταῦτα εἰπόντος αὐτοῦ (when he had said these things), dropped in the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And when he had said these things, the</mark> king, the governor, Bernice, and those who sat with them all rose up.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The</mark> king, the governor, Bernice, and those who sat with them all rose up.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐρρίψαμεν (we threw) with αὐτόχειρες; the CT reads ἐρρίψαν (they)" data-ref="Acts 27:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 27:19</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐρρίψαμεν (we threw) with αὐτόχειρες; the CT follows that reading's ἐρρίψαν (they)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">On the third day <mark class="chg">we</mark> threw the ship's tackle overboard with <mark class="chg">our</mark> own hands.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">On the third day <mark class="chg">they</mark> threw the ship's tackle overboard with <mark class="chg">their</mark> own hands.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ὁ ἑκατόνταρχος παρέδωκε τοὺς δεσμίους τῷ στρατοπεδάρχῃ; “the captain of the guard” renders στρατοπεδάρχης (cf. the New Korean Translation)" data-ref="Acts 28:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 28:16</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ὁ ἑκατόνταρχος παρέδωκε τοὺς δεσμίους τῷ στρατοπεδάρχῃ; “the captain of the guard” renders στρατοπεδάρχης (cf. the New Korean Translation)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now when we came to Rome, <mark class="chg">the centurion delivered the prisoners to the captain of the guard; but</mark> Paul was permitted to dwell by himself with the soldier who guarded him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now when we came to Rome, Paul was permitted to dwell by himself with the soldier who guarded him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT" data-ref="Acts 28:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 28:29</span><span class="srnote">printed (not present) in the CT; present in the RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And when he had said these words, the Jews departed and had a great dispute among themselves.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Romans</span><span class="bkcount">27</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “gospel” alone" data-ref="Romans 1:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:16</span><span class="srnote">RT τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “gospel” alone</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I am not ashamed of the <mark class="chg">gospel of Christ,</mark> for it is the power of God to salvation for everyone who believes, for the Jew first and also for the Greek.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I am not ashamed of the <mark class="chg">gospel,</mark> for it is the power of God to salvation for everyone who believes, for the Jew first and also for the Greek.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT list includes πορνείᾳ between ἀδικίᾳ and πονηρίᾳ; the CT omits “sexual immorality”" data-ref="Romans 1:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:29</span><span class="srnote">RT list includes πορνείᾳ between ἀδικίᾳ and πονηρίᾳ; the CT omits “sexual immorality”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">being filled with all unrighteousness, <mark class="chg">sexual immorality,</mark> wickedness, covetousness, maliciousness; full of envy, murder, strife, deceit, evil-mindedness; they are whisperers,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">being filled with all unrighteousness, wickedness, covetousness, maliciousness; full of envy, murder, strife, deceit, evil-mindedness; they are whisperers,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ἀσπόνδους (’implacable’) to the vice list" data-ref="Romans 1:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:31</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ἀσπόνδους ('implacable') to the vice list</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">without understanding, untrustworthy, unloving, <mark class="chg">unforgiving,</mark> unmerciful;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">without understanding, untrustworthy, unloving, unmerciful;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT οὐ κατενόησεν (he did not consider his own body dead); the CT has κατενόησεν (“knowing”)" data-ref="Romans 4:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 4:19</span><span class="srnote">RT οὐ κατενόησεν (he did not consider his own body dead); the CT prints “knowing”'s κατενόησεν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And being about a hundred years old, <mark class="chg">not considering</mark> his own body already dead and the deadness of Sarah's womb, he did not become weak in faith,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And being about a hundred years old, <mark class="chg">knowing that</mark> his own body <mark class="chg">was</mark> already dead and the deadness of Sarah's womb, he did not become weak in faith,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds μὴ κατὰ σάρκα περιπατοῦσιν ἀλλὰ κατὰ πνεῦμα" data-ref="Romans 8:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 8:1</span><span class="srnote">RT adds μὴ κατὰ σάρκα περιπατοῦσιν ἀλλὰ κατὰ πνεῦμα</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">There is therefore now no condemnation to those who are in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus, who do not walk according to the flesh, but according to the Spirit.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">There is therefore now no condemnation to those who are in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ηλευθερωσε με (set me free); the CT has “you”, following the critical ηλευθερωσεν σε" data-ref="Romans 8:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 8:2</span><span class="srnote">RT ηλευθερωσε με (set me free); the CT has “you”, following the critical ηλευθερωσεν σε</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For the law of the Spirit of life in Christ Jesus has made <mark class="chg">me</mark> free from the law of sin and death.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the law of the Spirit of life in Christ Jesus has made <mark class="chg">you</mark> free from the law of sin and death.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has λογον γαρ συντελων και συντεμνων εν δικαιοσυνη before the οτι clause; the CT omits the whole first half of the Isaiah 10:22-23 quotation" data-ref="Romans 9:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:28</span><span class="srnote">RT has λογον γαρ συντελων και συντεμνων εν δικαιοσυνη before the οτι clause; the CT omits the whole first half of the Isaiah 10:22-23 quotation</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For <mark class="chg">He will finish the work and cut it short in righteousness, because</mark> the Lord will make a short work upon the earth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the Lord will make a short work upon the earth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT repeats νομον δικαιοσυνης (law of righteousness); the CT has “the law” only for the second occurrence" data-ref="Romans 9:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:31</span><span class="srnote">RT repeats νομον δικαιοσυνης (law of righteousness); the CT has “the law” only for the second occurrence</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but Israel, pursuing the law of righteousness, has not attained to <mark class="chg">the law of righteousness.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but Israel, pursuing the law of righteousness, has not attained to <mark class="chg">that law.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ως εξ εργων νομου (works of the law); the CT has “works” only" data-ref="Romans 9:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:32</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ως εξ εργων νομου (works of the law); the CT has “works” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Why? Because they did not seek it by faith, but as it were, by <mark class="chg">the works of the law.</mark> They stumbled at that stumbling stone.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Why? Because they did not seek it by faith, but as it were, by <mark class="chg">works.</mark> They stumbled at that stumbling stone.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ειρηνην των ευαγγελιζομενων (who preach the gospel of peace), dropped by the CT" data-ref="Romans 10:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 10:15</span><span class="srnote">RT has ειρηνην των ευαγγελιζομενων (who preach the gospel of peace), dropped by the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And how shall they preach unless they are sent? As it is written: How beautiful are the feet of those who preach the <mark class="chg">gospel of peace!</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And how shall they preach unless they are sent? As it is written: How beautiful are the feet of those who preach the <mark class="chg">good news!</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ρηματος Θεου (word of God), not Χριστου (“of Christ”) as in the CT from the CT" data-ref="Romans 10:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 10:17</span><span class="srnote">RT ρηματος Θεου (word of God), not Χριστου (“of Christ”) as in the CT from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So then faith comes by hearing, and hearing by the word of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So then faith comes by hearing, and hearing by the word of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT carries the second, parallel half (ει δε εξ εργων, ουκετι εστι χαρις, επει το εργον ουκετι εστιν εργον), absent from the CT" data-ref="Romans 11:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 11:6</span><span class="srnote">RT carries the second, parallel half (ει δε εξ εργων, ουκετι εστι χαρις, επει το εργον ουκετι εστιν εργον), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And if by grace, then it is no longer of works; otherwise grace is no longer grace. <mark class="chg">But if it is of works, it is no longer grace; otherwise work is no longer work.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And if by grace, then it is no longer of works; otherwise grace is no longer grace.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ου ψευδομαρτυρησεις (you shall not bear false witness) between the theft and coveting commands; the CT drops it with the critical text" data-ref="Romans 13:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 13:9</span><span class="srnote">RT has ου ψευδομαρτυρησεις (you shall not bear false witness) between the theft and coveting commands; the CT drops it with the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For the commandments, You shall not commit adultery, You shall not murder, You shall not steal, <mark class="chg">You shall not bear false witness,</mark> You shall not covet, and if there is any other commandment, are all summed up in this saying, namely, You shall love your neighbor as yourself.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the commandments, You shall not commit adultery, You shall not murder, You shall not steal, You shall not covet, and if there is any other commandment, are all summed up in this saying, namely, You shall love your neighbor as yourself.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads δυνατος γαρ εστιν ο Θεος (God is able) rather than δυνατει ο κυριος; the CT has “to the Lord”, following the critical text" data-ref="Romans 14:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:4</span><span class="srnote">RT reads δυνατος γαρ εστιν ο Θεος (God is able) rather than δυνατει ο κυριος; the CT has “to the Lord”, following the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Who are you to judge another's servant? To his own master he stands or falls. Indeed, he will be made to stand, for <mark class="chg">God</mark> is able to make him stand.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Who are you to judge another's servant? To his own master he stands or falls. Indeed, he will be made to stand, for <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> is able to make him stand.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds και ο μη φρονων την ημεραν, Κυριω ου φρονει (he who does not regard the day, to the Lord he does not regard it), absent from the CT" data-ref="Romans 14:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:6</span><span class="srnote">RT adds και ο μη φρονων την ημεραν, Κυριω ου φρονει (he who does not regard the day, to the Lord he does not regard it), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He who observes the day, observes it to the <mark class="chg">Lord; and he who does not observe the day, to the Lord he does not observe it.</mark> He who eats, eats to the Lord, for he gives God thanks; and he who does not eat, to the Lord he does not eat, and gives God thanks.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He who observes the day, observes it to the <mark class="chg">Lord.</mark> He who eats, eats to the Lord, for he gives God thanks; and he who does not eat, to the Lord he does not eat, and gives God thanks.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT βηματος του Χριστου (judgment seat of Christ); the CT has “of God”, following the critical του Θεου" data-ref="Romans 14:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:10</span><span class="srnote">RT βηματος του Χριστου (judgment seat of Christ); the CT has “of God”, following the critical του Θεου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But why do you judge your brother? Or why do you show contempt for your brother? For we shall all stand before the judgment seat of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But why do you judge your brother? Or why do you show contempt for your brother? For we shall all stand before the judgment seat of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Ιησουν Χριστον (Jesus Christ); the CT has Christ only" data-ref="Romans 15:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Ιησουν Χριστον (Jesus Christ); the CT has Christ only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now I say that <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ has become a servant to the circumcision for the truth of God, to confirm the promises made to the fathers,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now I say that Christ has become a servant to the circumcision for the truth of God, to confirm the promises made to the fathers,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT opens with the vocative αδελφοι (“brethren”), which the CT drops" data-ref="Romans 15:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:15</span><span class="srnote">RT opens with the vocative αδελφοι (“brethren”), which the CT drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Nevertheless, brethren,</mark> I have written more boldly to you on some points, as reminding you, because of the grace given to me by God,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Nevertheless</mark> I have written more boldly to you on some points, as reminding you, because of the grace given to me by God,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads εν δυναμει πνευματος Θεου (the Spirit of God); the CT has “by the power of the Spirit” only" data-ref="Romans 15:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:19</span><span class="srnote">RT reads εν δυναμει πνευματος Θεου (the Spirit of God); the CT has “by the power of the Spirit” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">in mighty signs and wonders, by the power of the <mark class="chg">Spirit of God,</mark> so that from Jerusalem and round about to Illyricum I have fully preached the gospel of Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">in mighty signs and wonders, by the power of the <mark class="chg">Spirit,</mark> so that from Jerusalem and round about to Illyricum I have fully preached the gospel of Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ελευσομαι προς υμας (I will come to you) in this verse; the critical text carries the Spain clause in 15:23 instead, so the CT leaves this verse without its main verb" data-ref="Romans 15:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:24</span><span class="srnote">RT has ελευσομαι προς υμας (I will come to you) in this verse; the critical text carries the Spain clause in 15:23 instead, so the CT leaves this verse without its main verb</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Whenever I journey to Spain, I shall come to you.</mark> For I hope to see you as I pass by, and to be helped on my way there by you, if first I may enjoy your company for a while.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I hope to see you as I pass by, and to be helped on my way there by you, if first I may enjoy your company for a while.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ευλογιας του ευαγγελιου του Χριστου (blessing of the gospel of Christ); the CT omits “of the gospel”" data-ref="Romans 15:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:29</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ευλογιας του ευαγγελιου του Χριστου (blessing of the gospel of Christ); the CT omits “of the gospel”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But I know that when I come to you, I shall come in the fullness of the blessing of <mark class="chg">the gospel of</mark> Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But I know that when I come to you, I shall come in the fullness of the blessing of Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Πρίσκιλλαν, critical Πρίσκαν; the CT’s Prisca is the critical name" data-ref="Romans 16:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:3</span><span class="srnote">RT Πρίσκιλλαν, critical Πρίσκαν; the CT’s Prisca is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet <mark class="chg">Priscilla</mark> and Aquila, my fellow workers in Christ Jesus,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet <mark class="chg">Prisca</mark> and Aquila, my fellow workers in Christ Jesus,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Αχαιας (Achaia); the CT has “Asia”, following the critical Ασιας" data-ref="Romans 16:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:5</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Αχαιας (Achaia); the CT has “Asia”, following the critical Ασιας</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet the church that is in their house. Greet my beloved Epaenetus, who is the firstfruits of <mark class="chg">Achaia</mark> to Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet the church that is in their house. Greet my beloved Epaenetus, who is the firstfruits of <mark class="chg">Asia</mark> to Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads εις ημας (for us), not εις υμας (“for you”) as in the CT" data-ref="Romans 16:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:6</span><span class="srnote">RT reads εις ημας (for us), not εις υμας (“for you”) as in the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet Mary, who labored much for <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet Mary, who labored much for <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τω Κυριω ημων Ιησου Χριστω; the CT drops Jesus" data-ref="Romans 16:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:18</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τω Κυριω ημων Ιησου Χριστω; the CT drops Jesus</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For those who are such do not serve our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ, but their own belly, and by smooth words and flattering speech deceive the hearts of the simple.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For those who are such do not serve our Lord Christ, but their own belly, and by smooth words and flattering speech deceive the hearts of the simple.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the doxology with Αμην (Amen), absent from the CT" data-ref="Romans 16:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:20</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the doxology with Αμην (Amen), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the God of peace will crush Satan under your feet shortly. The grace of our Lord Jesus be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the God of peace will crush Satan under your feet shortly. The grace of our Lord Jesus be with you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (not present) in the CT; the RT repeats the grace benediction here" data-ref="Romans 16:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:24</span><span class="srnote">printed (not present) in the CT; the RT repeats the grace benediction here</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. <tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Corinthians</span><span class="bkcount">18</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads εβαπτισα (I baptized); the CT follows the critical εβαπτισθητε (you were baptized)" data-ref="1 Corinthians 1:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 1:15</span><span class="srnote">RT reads εβαπτισα (I baptized); the CT follows the critical εβαπτισθητε (you were baptized)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">lest anyone should say that <mark class="chg">I had</mark> baptized in my <mark class="chg">own</mark> name.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">lest anyone should say that <mark class="chg">you were</mark> baptized in my name.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Ελλησι (to the Greeks); the CT has “Gentile”, following the critical εθνεσιν" data-ref="1 Corinthians 1:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 1:23</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Ελλησι (to the Greeks); the CT has “Gentile”, following the critical εθνεσιν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but we preach Christ crucified, to the Jews a stumbling block and to the <mark class="chg">Greeks</mark> foolishness,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but we preach Christ crucified, to the Jews a stumbling block and to the <mark class="chg">Gentiles</mark> foolishness,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT lists three things, ζηλος και ερις και διχοστασιαι (envy, strife, divisions); the CT has “envy and strife” only" data-ref="1 Corinthians 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 3:3</span><span class="srnote">RT lists three things, ζηλος και ερις και διχοστασιαι (envy, strife, divisions); the CT has “envy and strife” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for you are still carnal. For where there <mark class="chg">are envy, strife,</mark> and <mark class="chg">divisions</mark> among you, are you not carnal and behaving like mere men?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for you are still carnal. For where there <mark class="chg">is envy</mark> and <mark class="chg">strife</mark> among you, are you not carnal and behaving like mere men?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου in both places; the CT drops Christ twice" data-ref="1 Corinthians 5:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 5:4</span><span class="srnote">RT reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου in both places; the CT drops Christ twice</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">In the name of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> when you are gathered together, along with my spirit, with the power of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">In the name of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> when you are gathered together, along with my spirit, with the power of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads υπερ ημων εθυθη (was sacrificed for us); the CT omits “for us”" data-ref="1 Corinthians 5:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 5:7</span><span class="srnote">RT reads υπερ ημων εθυθη (was sacrificed for us); the CT omits “for us”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore purge out the old leaven, that you may be a new lump, since you truly are unleavened. For indeed Christ, our Passover, was <mark class="chg">sacrificed for us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore purge out the old leaven, that you may be a new lump, since you truly are unleavened. For indeed Christ, our Passover, was <mark class="chg">sacrificed.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds και εν τω πνευματι υμων, ατινα εστι του Θεου (and in your spirit, which are God’s), dropped by the CT" data-ref="1 Corinthians 6:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 6:20</span><span class="srnote">RT adds και εν τω πνευματι υμων, ατινα εστι του Θεου (and in your spirit, which are God's), dropped by the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For you were bought at a price; therefore glorify God in your <mark class="chg">body and in your spirit, which are God's.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For you were bought at a price; therefore glorify God in your <mark class="chg">body.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads την οφειλομενην ευνοιαν (the affection due); the CT has “duty”, following the critical οφειλην" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:3</span><span class="srnote">RT reads την οφειλομενην ευνοιαν (the affection due); the CT has “duty”, following the critical οφειλην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let the husband render to his wife the <mark class="chg">affection due</mark> her, and likewise also the wife to her husband.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let the husband render to his wife the <mark class="chg">duty owed</mark> her, and likewise also the wife to her husband.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τη νηστεια και τη προσευχη (fasting and prayer); the CT has “prayer” only, following the critical text" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:5</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τη νηστεια και τη προσευχη (fasting and prayer); the CT has “prayer” only, following the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Do not deprive one another except with consent for a time, that you may give yourselves to <mark class="chg">fasting and</mark> prayer; and come together again so that Satan does not tempt you because of your lack of self-control.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Do not deprive one another except with consent for a time, that you may give yourselves to prayer; and come together again so that Satan does not tempt you because of your lack of self-control.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ως εμερισεν ο Θεος … ως κεκληκεν ο Κυριος; the CT swaps the two subjects (“the Lord apportioned” / “God called”)" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:17</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ως εμερισεν ο Θεος … ως κεκληκεν ο Κυριος; the CT swaps the two subjects (“the Lord apportioned” / “God called”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But as <mark class="chg">God</mark> has distributed to each one, as <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> has called each one, so let him walk. And so I ordain in all the churches.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But as <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> has distributed to each one, as <mark class="chg">God</mark> has called each one, so let him walk. And so I ordain in all the churches.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT συνειδήσει (’conscience’), not the critical συνηθείᾳ (’habit’)" data-ref="1 Corinthians 8:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 8:7</span><span class="srnote">RT συνειδήσει ('conscience'), not the critical συνηθείᾳ ('habit')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">However, there is not in everyone that knowledge; for some, <mark class="chg">with consciousness</mark> of the <mark class="chg">idol,</mark> until <mark class="chg">now</mark> eat it as a thing offered to an idol; and their conscience, being weak, is defiled.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">However, there is not in everyone that knowledge; for some, <mark class="chg">from habit</mark> of the <mark class="chg">idol</mark> until <mark class="chg">now,</mark> eat it as a thing offered to an idol; and their conscience, being weak, is defiled.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the gospel of Christ); the CT has only “gospel”" data-ref="1 Corinthians 9:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 9:18</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the gospel of Christ); the CT has only “gospel”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">What is my reward then? That when I preach the <mark class="chg">gospel of Christ,</mark> I may present the gospel without charge, that I may not abuse my authority in the gospel.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">What is my reward then? That when I preach the <mark class="chg">gospel,</mark> I may present the gospel without charge, that I may not abuse my authority in the gospel.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the verse τοῦ γὰρ Κυρίου ἡ γῆ καὶ τὸ πλήρωμα αὐτῆς (the earth is the Lord’s and its fullness); the CT has no equivalent" data-ref="1 Corinthians 10:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 10:28</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the verse τοῦ γὰρ Κυρίου ἡ γῆ καὶ τὸ πλήρωμα αὐτῆς (the earth is the Lord's and its fullness); the CT has no equivalent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if anyone says to you, This was offered to idols, do not eat it for the sake of the one who told you, and for conscience' <mark class="chg">sake; for the earth is the Lord's, and all its fullness.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if anyone says to you, This was offered to idols, do not eat it for the sake of the one who told you, and for conscience' <mark class="chg">sake.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT opens with the vocative ἀδελφοί (“brethren”), which the CT drops" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:2</span><span class="srnote">RT opens with the vocative ἀδελφοί (“brethren”), which the CT drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now I praise <mark class="chg">you, brethren,</mark> that you remember me in all things and keep the traditions just as I delivered them to you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now I praise <mark class="chg">you</mark> that you remember me in all things and keep the traditions just as I delivered them to you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has Λάβετε, φάγετε and τὸ ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν κλώμενον (take, eat; broken for you), both absent from the CT" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:24</span><span class="srnote">RT has Λάβετε, φάγετε and τὸ ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν κλώμενον (take, eat; broken for you), both absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and when He had given thanks, He broke it and said, <mark class="chg">Take, eat.</mark> This is My body which is <mark class="chg">broken</mark> for you; do this in remembrance of Me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and when He had given thanks, He broke it and said, This is My body which is for you; do this in remembrance of Me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἀναξίως (in an unworthy manner) in this verse; the CT keeps it only in v.27" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:29</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἀναξίως (in an unworthy manner) in this verse; the CT keeps it only in v.27</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For he who eats and <mark class="chg">drinks in an unworthy manner,</mark> not discerning the Lord's body, eats and drinks judgment to himself.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For he who eats and <mark class="chg">drinks,</mark> not discerning the Lord's body, eats and drinks judgment to himself.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ δεύτερος ἄνθρωπος ὁ Κύριος ἐξ οὐρανοῦ (the second Man is the Lord from heaven); the CT omits “the Lord”" data-ref="1 Corinthians 15:47"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 15:47</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ δεύτερος ἄνθρωπος ὁ Κύριος ἐξ οὐρανοῦ (the second Man is the Lord from heaven); the CT omits “the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The first man was of the earth, made of dust; the second Man is <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> from heaven.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The first man was of the earth, made of dust; the second Man is from heaven.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT order is Ποῦ σου, Θάνατε, τὸ κέντρον; Ποῦ σου, ᾍδη, τὸ νῖκος; — sting first, then Hades (“Hades”); the CT reverses the clauses and reads “O death” twice" data-ref="1 Corinthians 15:55"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 15:55</span><span class="srnote">RT order is Ποῦ σου, Θάνατε, τὸ κέντρον; Ποῦ σου, ᾍδη, τὸ νῖκος; — sting first, then Hades (“Hades”); the CT reverses the clauses and reads “O death” twice</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">O Death, where is your sting? <mark class="chg">O Hades, where is your victory?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">O Death, where is your victory?</mark> O Death, where is your sting?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τὸν Κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; the CT has only “the Lord”" data-ref="1 Corinthians 16:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 16:22</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τὸν Κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; the CT has only “the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If anyone does not love the <mark class="chg">Lord Jesus Christ,</mark> let him be accursed. O Lord, come!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If anyone does not love the <mark class="chg">Lord,</mark> let him be accursed. O Lord, come!</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Corinthians</span><span class="bkcount">15</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT repeats καὶ σωτηρίας in the second clause" data-ref="2 Corinthians 1:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 1:6</span><span class="srnote">RT repeats καὶ σωτηρίας in the second clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now if we are afflicted, it is for your comfort and salvation; and if we are comforted, it is for your <mark class="chg">comfort and salvation,</mark> which is effective for enduring the same sufferings which we also suffer.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now if we are afflicted, it is for your comfort and salvation; and if we are comforted, it is for your <mark class="chg">comfort,</mark> which is effective for enduring the same sufferings which we also suffer.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἐν ἁπλότητι καὶ εἰλικρινείᾳ Θεοῦ (simplicity/“sincerity”); the CT follows the critical ἁγιότητι (“holiness”)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἐν ἁπλότητι καὶ εἰλικρινείᾳ Θεοῦ (simplicity/“sincerity”); the CT follows the critical ἁγιότητι (“holiness”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For our boasting is this: the testimony of our conscience that we conducted ourselves in the world in <mark class="chg">simplicity</mark> and godly sincerity, not with fleshly wisdom but by the grace of God, and more abundantly toward you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For our boasting is this: the testimony of our conscience that we conducted ourselves in the world in <mark class="chg">holiness</mark> and godly sincerity, not with fleshly wisdom but by the grace of God, and more abundantly toward you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τὴν νέκρωσιν τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the dying of the Lord Jesus); the CT has only Jesus" data-ref="2 Corinthians 4:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 4:10</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τὴν νέκρωσιν τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the dying of the Lord Jesus); the CT has only Jesus</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">always carrying about in the body the dying of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> Jesus, that the life of Jesus also may be manifested in our body.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">always carrying about in the body the dying of Jesus, that the life of Jesus also may be manifested in our body.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads γέγονε καινὰ τὰ πάντα (all things have become new); the CT drops τὰ πάντα" data-ref="2 Corinthians 5:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 5:17</span><span class="srnote">RT reads γέγονε καινὰ τὰ πάντα (all things have become new); the CT drops τὰ πάντα</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore, if anyone is in Christ, he is a new creation; old things have passed away; behold, <mark class="chg">all things have become new.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore, if anyone is in Christ, he is a new creation; old things have passed away; behold, <mark class="chg">the new has come.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has only Christ" data-ref="2 Corinthians 5:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 5:18</span><span class="srnote">RT reads διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has only Christ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now all things are of God, who has reconciled us to Himself through <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ and has given us the ministry of reconciliation,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now all things are of God, who has reconciled us to Himself through Christ and has given us the ministry of reconciliation,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὑμεῖς γὰρ ναὸς Θεοῦ ἐστε ζῶντος (you are); the CT follows the critical ἐσμεν (“we”)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 6:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 6:16</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὑμεῖς γὰρ ναὸς Θεοῦ ἐστε ζῶντος (you are); the CT follows the critical ἐσμεν (“we”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And what agreement has the temple of God with idols? For <mark class="chg">you</mark> are the temple of the living God. As God has said: I will dwell in them and walk among them; I will be their God, and they shall be My people.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And what agreement has the temple of God with idols? For <mark class="chg">we</mark> are the temple of the living God. As God has said: I will dwell in them and walk among them; I will be their God, and they shall be My people.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds τῆς καυχήσεως (’of this boasting’)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 9:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 9:4</span><span class="srnote">RT adds τῆς καυχήσεως ('of this boasting')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">lest if some Macedonians come with me and find you unprepared, we, not to mention you, should be ashamed of this <mark class="chg">confident boasting.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">lest if some Macedonians come with me and find you unprepared, we, not to mention you, should be ashamed of this <mark class="chg">confidence.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes οὕτως καὶ ἡμεῖς Χριστοῦ (so we also are Christ’s); the CT keeps only “we also are such”" data-ref="2 Corinthians 10:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 10:7</span><span class="srnote">RT closes οὕτως καὶ ἡμεῖς Χριστοῦ (so we also are Christ's); the CT keeps only “we also are such”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Do you look at things according to the outward appearance? If anyone is convinced in himself that he is Christ's, let him again consider this in himself, that just as he is Christ's, even so we also <mark class="chg">are Christ's.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Do you look at things according to the outward appearance? If anyone is convinced in himself that he is Christ's, let him again consider this in himself, that just as he is Christ's, even so we also <mark class="chg">are.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἀπὸ τῆς ἁπλότητος τῆς εἰς τὸν Χριστόν only; the critical καὶ τῆς ἁγνότητος behind “purity” is absent" data-ref="2 Corinthians 11:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 11:3</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἀπὸ τῆς ἁπλότητος τῆς εἰς τὸν Χριστόν only; the critical καὶ τῆς ἁγνότητος behind “purity” is absent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But I fear, lest somehow, as the serpent deceived Eve by his craftiness, so your minds may be corrupted from the simplicity of devotion to Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But I fear, lest somehow, as the serpent deceived Eve by his craftiness, so your minds may be corrupted from the simplicity <mark class="chg">and purity</mark> of devotion to Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦ Πατρὸς τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT omits Christ" data-ref="2 Corinthians 11:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 11:31</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦ Πατρὸς τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT omits Christ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The God and Father of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> who is blessed forever, knows that I am not lying.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The God and Father of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> who is blessed forever, knows that I am not lying.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT καυχᾶσθαι δή (’boasting indeed’), not the critical δεῖ (’it is necessary’)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 12:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 12:1</span><span class="srnote">RT καυχᾶσθαι δή ('boasting indeed'), not the critical δεῖ ('it is necessary')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Though it is not profitable, I <mark class="chg">do</mark> boast; I will speak of visions and revelations of the Lord.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Though it is not profitable, I <mark class="chg">must needs</mark> boast; I will speak of visions and revelations of the Lord.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="the kiss clause is RT 13:12, not part of 13:11" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:11</span><span class="srnote">the kiss clause is RT 13:12, not part of 13:11</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Finally, brethren, rejoice. Become complete. Be of good comfort, be of one mind, live in peace; and the God of love and peace will be with you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Finally, brethren, rejoice. Become complete. Be of good comfort, be of one mind, live in peace; and the God of love and peace will be with you. <mark class="chg">Greet one another with a holy kiss.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT 13:12; the CT folded this into 13:11" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:12</span><span class="srnote">RT 13:12; the CT folded this into 13:11</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Greet one another with a holy kiss.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">All the saints greet you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT 13:13; the grace benediction moves to a new 13:14" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:13</span><span class="srnote">RT 13:13; the grace benediction moves to a new 13:14</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">All</mark> the <mark class="chg">saints greet you.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The grace of</mark> the <mark class="chg">Lord Jesus Christ, and the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit be with you all.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT numbers the grace benediction as 13:14; the CT numbers it 13:13" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:14</span><span class="srnote">RT numbers the grace benediction as 13:14; the CT numbers it 13:13</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="rest">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The grace of the Lord Jesus Christ, and the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit be with you all.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Galatians</span><span class="bkcount">13</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὅτε δὲ εὐδόκησεν ὁ Θεός (when God was pleased) — the subject God is dropped in the CT" data-ref="Galatians 1:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 1:15</span><span class="srnote">RT ὅτε δὲ εὐδόκησεν ὁ Θεός (when God was pleased) — the subject God is dropped in the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But when it pleased <mark class="chg">God,</mark> who set me apart from my mother's womb and called me through His grace,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But when it pleased <mark class="chg">Him</mark> who set me apart from my mother's womb and called me through His grace,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἱστορῆσαι Πέτρον (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶν behind “Cephas”" data-ref="Galatians 1:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 1:18</span><span class="srnote">RT ἱστορῆσαι Πέτρον (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶν behind “Cephas”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then after three years I went up to Jerusalem to see <mark class="chg">Peter,</mark> and remained with him fifteen days.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then after three years I went up to Jerusalem to see <mark class="chg">Cephas,</mark> and remained with him fifteen days.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὅτε δὲ ἦλθεν Πέτρος (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶς behind “Cephas”" data-ref="Galatians 2:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 2:11</span><span class="srnote">RT ὅτε δὲ ἦλθεν Πέτρος (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶς behind “Cephas”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now when <mark class="chg">Peter had come</mark> to Antioch, I withstood him to his face, because he was to be blamed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now when <mark class="chg">Cephas came</mark> to Antioch, I withstood him to his face, because he was to be blamed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT εἶπεν τῷ Πέτρῳ (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾷ behind “Cephas”" data-ref="Galatians 2:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 2:14</span><span class="srnote">RT εἶπεν τῷ Πέτρῳ (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾷ behind “Cephas”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But when I saw that they were not straightforward about the truth of the gospel, I said to <mark class="chg">Peter</mark> before them all, If you, being a Jew, live in the manner of Gentiles and not as the Jews, why do you compel Gentiles to live as Jews?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But when I saw that they were not straightforward about the truth of the gospel, I said to <mark class="chg">Cephas</mark> before them all, If you, being a Jew, live in the manner of Gentiles and not as the Jews, why do you compel Gentiles to live as Jews?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ἐν ὑμῖν (’among you’) and τῇ ἀληθεία μὴ πείθεσθαι (’not obey the truth’)" data-ref="Galatians 3:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 3:1</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ἐν ὑμῖν ('among you') and τῇ ἀληθεία μὴ πείθεσθαι ('not obey the truth')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">O foolish Galatians! Who has bewitched <mark class="chg">you that you should not obey the truth,</mark> before whose eyes Jesus Christ was clearly portrayed <mark class="chg">among you</mark> as crucified?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">O foolish Galatians! Who has bewitched <mark class="chg">you,</mark> before whose eyes Jesus Christ was clearly portrayed as crucified?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὴν διαθήκην τὴν προκεκυρωμένην ὑπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ εἰς Χριστόν (confirmed of God in Christ); the CT omits “in Christ”" data-ref="Galatians 3:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 3:17</span><span class="srnote">RT τὴν διαθήκην τὴν προκεκυρωμένην ὑπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ εἰς Χριστόν (confirmed of God in Christ); the CT omits “in Christ”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And this I say, that the law, which was four hundred and thirty years later, cannot annul the covenant that was confirmed before by <mark class="chg">God in Christ,</mark> so as to make the promise of no effect.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And this I say, that the law, which was four hundred and thirty years later, cannot annul the covenant that was confirmed before by <mark class="chg">God,</mark> so as to make the promise of no effect.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT κληρονόμος Θεοῦ διὰ Χριστοῦ; the CT follows the critical διὰ θεοῦ (“through God”)" data-ref="Galatians 4:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:7</span><span class="srnote">RT κληρονόμος Θεοῦ διὰ Χριστοῦ; the CT follows the critical διὰ θεοῦ (“through God”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore you are no longer a slave but a son, and if a son, then an heir <mark class="chg">of God</mark> through <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore you are no longer a slave but a son, and if a son, then an heir through <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τίς (’what then was’), not the critical ποῦ (’where’)" data-ref="Galatians 4:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:15</span><span class="srnote">RT τίς ('what then was'), not the critical ποῦ ('where')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">What</mark> then <mark class="chg">has become of</mark> your blessing? For I bear you witness that, if possible, you would have plucked out your own eyes and given them to me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Where</mark> then <mark class="chg">is</mark> your blessing? For I bear you witness that, if possible, you would have plucked out your own eyes and given them to me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μήτηρ πάντων ἡμῶν (mother of us all); the CT has only “our mother”" data-ref="Galatians 4:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:26</span><span class="srnote">RT μήτηρ πάντων ἡμῶν (mother of us all); the CT has only “our mother”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but the Jerusalem above is free, which is <mark class="chg">the mother of us all.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but the Jerusalem above is free, which is <mark class="chg">our mother.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἅτινά ἐστι μοιχεία, πορνεία, ἀκαθαρσία, ἀσέλγεια — μοιχεία (“adultery”) is absent from the CT" data-ref="Galatians 5:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 5:19</span><span class="srnote">RT ἅτινά ἐστι μοιχεία, πορνεία, ἀκαθαρσία, ἀσέλγεια — μοιχεία (“adultery”) is absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now the works of the flesh are evident, which are: <mark class="chg">adultery,</mark> fornication, uncleanness, lewdness,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now the works of the flesh are evident, which are: fornication, uncleanness, lewdness,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT φθόνοι, φόνοι, μέθαι — φόνοι (“murder”) is absent from the CT" data-ref="Galatians 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 5:21</span><span class="srnote">RT φθόνοι, φόνοι, μέθαι — φόνοι (“murder”) is absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">envy, <mark class="chg">murders,</mark> drunkenness, revelries, and the like; of which I tell you beforehand, just as I also told you in time past, that those who practice such things will not inherit the kingdom of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">envy, drunkenness, revelries, and the like; of which I tell you beforehand, just as I also told you in time past, that those who practice such things will not inherit the kingdom of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν γὰρ Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ οὔτε περιτομή τι ἰσχύει; the opening “in Christ Jesus” is missing" data-ref="Galatians 6:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 6:15</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν γὰρ Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ οὔτε περιτομή τι ἰσχύει; the opening “in Christ Jesus” is missing</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For <mark class="chg">in Christ Jesus</mark> neither circumcision nor uncircumcision <mark class="chg">avails</mark> anything, but a new creation.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For neither circumcision nor uncircumcision <mark class="chg">is</mark> anything, but a new creation.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὰ στίγματα τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the marks of the Lord Jesus); the CT keeps only Jesus" data-ref="Galatians 6:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 6:17</span><span class="srnote">RT τὰ στίγματα τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the marks of the Lord Jesus); the CT keeps only Jesus</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">From now on let no one trouble me, for I bear in my body the marks of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> Jesus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">From now on let no one trouble me, for I bear in my body the marks of Jesus.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Ephesians</span><span class="bkcount">14</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς τῆς διανοίας (understanding), not the critical τῆς καρδίας behind “of the heart”" data-ref="Ephesians 1:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 1:18</span><span class="srnote">RT τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς τῆς διανοίας (understanding), not the critical τῆς καρδίας behind “of the heart”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">the eyes of your <mark class="chg">understanding</mark> being enlightened, that you may know what is the hope of His calling, and what are the riches of the glory of His inheritance in the saints,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">the eyes of your <mark class="chg">heart</mark> being enlightened, that you may know what is the hope of His calling, and what are the riches of the glory of His inheritance in the saints,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT εὐηγγελίσατο εἰρήνην once; the CT doubles “peace” from the critical double εἰρήνην" data-ref="Ephesians 2:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 2:17</span><span class="srnote">RT εὐηγγελίσατο εἰρήνην once; the CT doubles “peace” from the critical double εἰρήνην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And He came and preached peace to you who were afar off and to those who were near.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And He came and preached peace to you who were afar off and <mark class="chg">peace</mark> to those who were near.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν τῷ Χριστῷ (in Christ); the Jesus of “Christ Jesus” is the critical Ἰησοῦ" data-ref="Ephesians 3:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:6</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν τῷ Χριστῷ (in Christ); the Jesus of “Christ Jesus” is the critical Ἰησοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that the Gentiles should be fellow heirs, of the same body, and partakers of His promise in Christ through the gospel,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that the Gentiles should be fellow heirs, of the same body, and partakers of His promise in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> through the gospel,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τίς ἡ κοινωνία τοῦ μυστηρίου (the fellowship of the mystery) and τῷ τὰ πάντα κτίσαντι διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “dispensation” and no “through Jesus Christ”" data-ref="Ephesians 3:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:9</span><span class="srnote">RT τίς ἡ κοινωνία τοῦ μυστηρίου (the fellowship of the mystery) and τῷ τὰ πάντα κτίσαντι διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “dispensation” and no “through Jesus Christ”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and to make all see what is the <mark class="chg">fellowship</mark> of the mystery which has been hidden from the ages in God who created all <mark class="chg">things through Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and to make all see what is the <mark class="chg">dispensation</mark> of the mystery which has been hidden from the ages in God who created all <mark class="chg">things,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ" data-ref="Ephesians 3:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:14</span><span class="srnote">RT has τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For this reason I bow my knees to <mark class="chg">the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For this reason I bow my knees to <mark class="chg">every family in heaven and on earth,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="re-cut so that 3:14 carries ’to the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ’" data-ref="Ephesians 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:15</span><span class="srnote">re-cut so that 3:14 carries 'to the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ'</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">to</mark> the Father <mark class="chg">who gives name to</mark> every family in heaven and on <mark class="chg">earth,</mark> I pray,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">before</mark> the Father <mark class="chg">from whom</mark> every family in heaven and on <mark class="chg">earth is named,</mark> I <mark class="chg">bow my knees and</mark> pray,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT καὶ ἐν πᾶσιν ὑμῖν (and in you all); the CT’s “in all” drops the ὑμῖν" data-ref="Ephesians 4:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:6</span><span class="srnote">RT καὶ ἐν πᾶσιν ὑμῖν (and in you all); the CT’s “in all” drops the ὑμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">one God and Father of all, who is above all, and through all, and in <mark class="chg">you</mark> all.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">one God and Father of all, who is above all, and through all, and in all.</span></span></tr>
