<tr class="tr" data-note="RT καὶ κατέβη πρῶτον (first descended); the CT omits “first”" data-ref="Ephesians 4:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:9</span><span class="srnote">RT καὶ κατέβη πρῶτον (first descended); the CT omits “first”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now this, that He ascended, what does it mean but that He also <mark class="chg">first</mark> descended into the lower parts of the earth?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now this, that He ascended, what does it mean but that He also descended into the lower parts of the earth?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT καθὼς καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ ἔθνη (as the rest of the Gentiles); the CT has only “Gentile”" data-ref="Ephesians 4:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:17</span><span class="srnote">RT καθὼς καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ ἔθνη (as the rest of the Gentiles); the CT has only “Gentile”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This I say, therefore, and testify in the Lord, that you should no longer walk as the <mark class="chg">rest of the</mark> Gentiles walk, in the futility of their mind,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This I say, therefore, and testify in the Lord, that you should no longer walk as the Gentiles walk, in the futility of their mind,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὁ καρπὸς τοῦ Πνεύματος (fruit of the Spirit), not the critical τοῦ φωτός behind “of light”" data-ref="Ephesians 5:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:9</span><span class="srnote">RT ὁ καρπὸς τοῦ Πνεύματος (fruit of the Spirit), not the critical τοῦ φωτός behind “of light”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for the fruit of <mark class="chg">the Spirit</mark> is in all goodness, righteousness, and truth,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for the fruit of <mark class="chg">light</mark> is in all goodness, righteousness, and truth,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν φόβῳ Θεοῦ (fear of God), not the critical Χριστοῦ behind “Christ”" data-ref="Ephesians 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:21</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν φόβῳ Θεοῦ (fear of God), not the critical Χριστοῦ behind “Christ”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">submitting to one another in the fear of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">submitting to one another in the fear of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἐκ τῆς σαρκὸς αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ τῶν ὀστεων αὐτοῦ (of His flesh and of His bones); the CT drops the clause" data-ref="Ephesians 5:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:30</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἐκ τῆς σαρκὸς αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ τῶν ὀστεων αὐτοῦ (of His flesh and of His bones); the CT drops the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For we are members of His <mark class="chg">body, of His flesh and of His bones.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For we are members of His <mark class="chg">body.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Τὸ λοιπόν, ἀδελφοί μου (Finally, my brethren); the CT omits the address “brethren”" data-ref="Ephesians 6:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 6:10</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Τὸ λοιπόν, ἀδελφοί μου (Finally, my brethren); the CT omits the address “brethren”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Finally, <mark class="chg">my brethren,</mark> be strong in the Lord and in the power of His might.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Finally, be strong in the Lord and in the power of His might.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT" data-ref="Ephesians 6:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 6:24</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Grace be with all those who love our Lord Jesus Christ in sincerity. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Grace be with all those who love our Lord Jesus Christ in sincerity.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Philippians</span><span class="bkcount">6</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT v16 is the ἐριθείας clause (selfish ambition); the CT puts the ἀγάπης clause first and so swaps vv.16-17" data-ref="Philippians 1:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 1:16</span><span class="srnote">RT v16 is the ἐριθείας clause (selfish ambition); the CT puts the ἀγάπης clause first and so swaps vv.16-17</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The <mark class="chg">former preach Christ from selfish ambition, not sincerely, supposing to add affliction to my chains.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The <mark class="chg">latter do it out of love, knowing that I am appointed for the defense of the gospel.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT v17 is the ἀγάπης clause (out of love); the CT carries the CT’s reversed order" data-ref="Philippians 1:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 1:17</span><span class="srnote">RT v17 is the ἀγάπης clause (out of love); the CT carries the critical text's reversed order</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">But the latter do it</mark> out of <mark class="chg">love, knowing that I am appointed for the defense of the gospel.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The others preach Christ</mark> out of <mark class="chg">selfish ambition, not sincerely, supposing to add affliction to my chains.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πνεύματι Θεῷ (’worship God in the Spirit’), not the critical πνεύματι θεοῦ" data-ref="Philippians 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 3:3</span><span class="srnote">RT πνεύματι Θεῷ ('worship God in the Spirit'), not the critical πνεύματι θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For we are the circumcision, who <mark class="chg">worship God in</mark> the <mark class="chg">Spirit,</mark> rejoice in Christ Jesus, and have no confidence in the flesh,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For we are the circumcision, who <mark class="chg">serve by</mark> the <mark class="chg">Spirit of God and</mark> rejoice in Christ Jesus, and have no confidence in the flesh,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds κανόνι, τὸ αὐτὸ φρονεῖν (by the same rule, be of the same mind), absent from the CT" data-ref="Philippians 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 3:16</span><span class="srnote">RT adds κανόνι, τὸ αὐτὸ φρονεῖν (by the same rule, be of the same mind), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nevertheless, to whatever extent we have already attained, let us walk by the same <mark class="chg">rule and be of the same mind.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nevertheless, to whatever extent we have already attained, let us walk by the same <mark class="chg">rule.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἐν τῷ ἐνδυναμοῦντί με Χριστῷ; the CT drops Χριστῷ, leaving the strengthener unnamed" data-ref="Philippians 4:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 4:13</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἐν τῷ ἐνδυναμοῦντί με Χριστῷ; the CT drops Χριστῷ, leaving the strengthener unnamed</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I can do all things through <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> who strengthens me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I can do all things through <mark class="chg">Him</mark> who strengthens me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads μετὰ πάντων ὑμῶν (with you all) plus Ἀμήν; the CT follows the critical μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματος ὑμῶν (“with your spirit”) and has no Amen" data-ref="Philippians 4:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 4:23</span><span class="srnote">RT reads μετὰ πάντων ὑμῶν (with you all) plus Ἀμήν; the CT follows the critical μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματος ὑμῶν (“with your spirit”) and has no Amen</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with <mark class="chg">you all. Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with <mark class="chg">your spirit.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Colossians</span><span class="bkcount">14</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT drops the second source, “our Lord Jesus Christ”" data-ref="Colossians 1:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:2</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT drops the second source, “our Lord Jesus Christ”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To the saints and faithful brethren in Christ who are in Colosse: Grace to you and peace from God our <mark class="chg">Father and the Lord Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To the saints and faithful brethren in Christ who are in Colosse: Grace to you and peace from God our <mark class="chg">Father.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἔστι καρποφορούμενον only; αὐξανόμενον (“growing”) is an addition kept by the CT" data-ref="Colossians 1:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:6</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἔστι καρποφορούμενον only; αὐξανόμενον (“growing”) is an addition kept by the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">which has come to you, as it is also in all the world, and is bearing <mark class="chg">fruit,</mark> as it also does among you, since the day you heard and knew the grace of God in truth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">which has come to you, as it is also in all the world, and is bearing <mark class="chg">fruit and increasing,</mark> as it also does among you, since the day you heard and knew the grace of God in truth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has διὰ τοῦ αἵματος αὐτοῦ (through His blood), absent from the CT" data-ref="Colossians 1:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:14</span><span class="srnote">RT has διὰ τοῦ αἵματος αὐτοῦ (through His blood), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">in whom we have <mark class="chg">redemption through His blood,</mark> the forgiveness of sins.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">in whom we have <mark class="chg">redemption,</mark> the forgiveness of sins.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τοῦ μυστηρίου τοῦ Θεοῦ καὶ πατρὸς καὶ τοῦ Χριστοῦ (God, both the Father and Christ); the CT drops “the Father”" data-ref="Colossians 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:2</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τοῦ μυστηρίου τοῦ Θεοῦ καὶ πατρὸς καὶ τοῦ Χριστοῦ (God, both the Father and Christ); the CT drops “the Father”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that their hearts may be encouraged, being knit together in love, and attaining to all riches of the full assurance of understanding, to the knowledge of the mystery of God, <mark class="chg">both of the Father and of</mark> Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that their hearts may be encouraged, being knit together in love, and attaining to all riches of the full assurance of understanding, to the knowledge of the mystery of God, <mark class="chg">which is</mark> Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads τοῦ σώματος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν τῆς σαρκός (the body of the sins of the flesh); the CT drops τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν (“sin”)" data-ref="Colossians 2:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:11</span><span class="srnote">RT reads τοῦ σώματος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν τῆς σαρκός (the body of the sins of the flesh); the CT drops τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν (“sin”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">In Him you were also circumcised with the circumcision made without hands, by putting off the body of the <mark class="chg">sins of the</mark> flesh, by the circumcision of Christ,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">In Him you were also circumcised with the circumcision made without hands, by putting off the body of the flesh, by the circumcision of Christ,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads χαρισάμενος ὑμῖν (forgiven you), not the critical ἡμῖν that the CT renders “our”" data-ref="Colossians 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:13</span><span class="srnote">RT reads χαρισάμενος ὑμῖν (forgiven you), not the critical ἡμῖν that the CT renders “our”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And you, being dead in your trespasses and the uncircumcision of your flesh, He has made alive together with Him, having forgiven <mark class="chg">you</mark> all trespasses,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And you, being dead in your trespasses and the uncircumcision of your flesh, He has made alive together with Him, having forgiven <mark class="chg">us</mark> all trespasses,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ Χριστός (Christ), not the critical ὁ κύριος that the CT renders “the Lord”" data-ref="Colossians 3:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:13</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ Χριστός (Christ), not the critical ὁ κύριος that the CT renders “the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">bearing with one another, and forgiving one another, if anyone has a complaint against another; even as <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> forgave you, so you also must do.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">bearing with one another, and forgiving one another, if anyone has a complaint against another; even as <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> forgave you, so you also must do.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἡ εἰρήνη τοῦ Θεοῦ (the peace of God); the CT follows the critical τοῦ Χριστοῦ (“the peace of Christ”)" data-ref="Colossians 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:15</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἡ εἰρήνη τοῦ Θεοῦ (the peace of God); the CT follows the critical τοῦ Χριστοῦ (“the peace of Christ”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And let the peace of <mark class="chg">God</mark> rule in your hearts, to which also you were called in one body; and be thankful.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And let the peace of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> rule in your hearts, to which also you were called in one body; and be thankful.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ᾄδοντες … τῷ Κυρίῳ (singing to the Lord); the CT follows the critical τῷ θεῷ (“singing to God”)" data-ref="Colossians 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:16</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ᾄδοντες … τῷ Κυρίῳ (singing to the Lord); the CT follows the critical τῷ θεῷ (“singing to God”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let the word of Christ dwell in you richly in all wisdom, teaching and admonishing one another in psalms and hymns and spiritual songs, singing with grace in your hearts to <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let the word of Christ dwell in you richly in all wisdom, teaching and admonishing one another in psalms and hymns and spiritual songs, singing with grace in your hearts to <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads φοβούμενοι τὸν Θεόν (fearing God); the CT follows the critical τὸν κύριον (“fearing the Lord”)" data-ref="Colossians 3:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:22</span><span class="srnote">RT reads φοβούμενοι τὸν Θεόν (fearing God); the CT follows the critical τὸν κύριον (“fearing the Lord”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Bondservants, obey in all things your masters according to the flesh, not with eyeservice, as men-pleasers, but in sincerity of heart, fearing <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Bondservants, obey in all things your masters according to the flesh, not with eyeservice, as men-pleasers, but in sincerity of heart, fearing <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἵνα γνῷ τὰ περὶ ὑμῶν (that he may know your circumstances); the CT follows the critical γνῶτε and reverses subject and object (“that you may know our circumstances”)" data-ref="Colossians 4:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἵνα γνῷ τὰ περὶ ὑμῶν (that he may know your circumstances); the CT follows the critical γνῶτε and reverses subject and object (“that you may know our circumstances”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I am sending him to you for this very purpose, that <mark class="chg">he</mark> may know <mark class="chg">your</mark> circumstances and comfort your hearts.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I am sending him to you for this very purpose, that <mark class="chg">you</mark> may know <mark class="chg">our</mark> circumstances and <mark class="chg">that he may</mark> comfort your hearts.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τέλειοι καὶ πεπληρωμένοι (’perfect and filled’), not πεπληροφορημένοι (’fully assured’)" data-ref="Colossians 4:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:12</span><span class="srnote">RT τέλειοι καὶ πεπληρωμένοι ('perfect and filled'), not πεπληροφορημένοι ('fully assured')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Epaphras, who is one of your number, a servant of Christ Jesus, greets you, always laboring fervently for you in prayers, that you may stand perfect and <mark class="chg">complete</mark> in all the will of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Epaphras, who is one of your number, a servant of Christ Jesus, greets you, always laboring fervently for you in prayers, that you may stand perfect and <mark class="chg">fully assured</mark> in all the will of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ζῆλον πολύν (great zeal), not the critical πόνον that the CT renders “much labour”" data-ref="Colossians 4:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:13</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ζῆλον πολύν (great zeal), not the critical πόνον that the CT renders “much labour”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I bear him witness that he <mark class="chg">has a great zeal</mark> for you, and for those who are in Laodicea, and those in Hierapolis.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I bear him witness that he <mark class="chg">labors much</mark> for you, and for those who are in Laodicea, and those in Hierapolis.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT" data-ref="Colossians 4:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:18</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This salutation by my own hand—Paul. Remember my chains. Grace be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This salutation by my own hand—Paul. Remember my chains. Grace be with you.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Thessalonians</span><span class="bkcount">5</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ after the greeting; the CT drops the source of grace and peace" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 1:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 1:1</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ after the greeting; the CT drops the source of grace and peace</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Paul, Silvanus, and Timothy, To the church of the Thessalonians in God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ: Grace to you and <mark class="chg">peace from God our Father and the Lord Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Paul, Silvanus, and Timothy, To the church of the Thessalonians in God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ: Grace to you and <mark class="chg">peace.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has καὶ συνεργὸν ἡμῶν (and our fellow laborer); the CT drops “our fellow labourer”" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 3:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 3:2</span><span class="srnote">RT has καὶ συνεργὸν ἡμῶν (and our fellow laborer); the CT drops “our fellow labourer”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and sent Timothy, our brother and minister of <mark class="chg">God, and our fellow laborer</mark> in the gospel of Christ, to establish you and encourage you concerning your faith,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and sent Timothy, our brother and minister of <mark class="chg">God</mark> in the gospel of Christ, to establish you and encourage you concerning your faith,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads εἰς ἡμᾶς (given to us), not the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς that the CT renders “to you”" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 4:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 4:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads εἰς ἡμᾶς (given to us), not the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς that the CT renders “to you”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore he who rejects this does not reject man, but God, who has also given His Holy Spirit to <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore he who rejects this does not reject man, but God, who has also given His Holy Spirit to <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads πᾶσι τοῖς ἁγίοις ἀδελφοῖς (all the holy brethren); the CT drops ἁγίοις" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 5:27"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 5:27</span><span class="srnote">RT reads πᾶσι τοῖς ἁγίοις ἀδελφοῖς (all the holy brethren); the CT drops ἁγίοις</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you by the Lord that this epistle be read to all the <mark class="chg">holy</mark> brethren.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you by the Lord that this epistle be read to all the brethren.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 5:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 5:28</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Thessalonians</span><span class="bkcount">5</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἡμέρα τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the day of Christ), not the critical τοῦ κυρίου that the CT renders “the day of the Lord”" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:2</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἡμέρα τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the day of Christ), not the critical τοῦ κυρίου that the CT renders “the day of the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that you not be soon shaken in mind or troubled, either by spirit or by word or by letter, as if from us, as though the day of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> had come.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that you not be soon shaken in mind or troubled, either by spirit or by word or by letter, as if from us, as though the day of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> had come.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ ἄνθρωπος τῆς ἁμαρτίας (the man of sin); the CT follows the critical τῆς ἀνομίας (“the man of lawlessness”)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:3</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ ἄνθρωπος τῆς ἁμαρτίας (the man of sin); the CT follows the critical τῆς ἀνομίας (“the man of lawlessness”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let no one deceive you by any means; for that Day will not come unless the falling away comes first, and the man of <mark class="chg">sin</mark> is revealed, the son of perdition,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let no one deceive you by any means; for that Day will not come unless the falling away comes first, and the man of <mark class="chg">lawlessness</mark> is revealed, the son of perdition,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ὡς Θεὸν καθίσαι (sit as God); the CT drops ὡς θεόν" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:4</span><span class="srnote">RT has ὡς Θεὸν καθίσαι (sit as God); the CT drops ὡς θεόν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">who opposes and exalts himself above all that is called God or that is worshiped, so that he sits <mark class="chg">as God</mark> in the temple of God, showing himself that he is God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">who opposes and exalts himself above all that is called God or that is worshiped, so that he sits in the temple of God, showing himself that he is God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads διὰ τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (through our Lord Jesus Christ); the CT follows the critical ἐν κυρίῳ (“in”)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 3:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 3:12</span><span class="srnote">RT reads διὰ τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (through our Lord Jesus Christ); the CT follows the critical ἐν κυρίῳ (“in”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now those who are such we command and exhort <mark class="chg">through our</mark> Lord Jesus Christ that they work in quietness and eat their own bread.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now those who are such we command and exhort <mark class="chg">in the</mark> Lord Jesus Christ that they work in quietness and eat their own bread.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the epistle with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it (the Πρὸς Θεσσαλονικεῖς colophon is a subscription, not verse text)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 3:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 3:18</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the epistle with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it (the Πρὸς Θεσσαλονικεῖς colophon is a subscription, not verse text)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Timothy</span><span class="bkcount">12</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μόνῳ σοφῷ Θεῷ (alone wise God); the CT’s “who alone is” drops σοφῷ" data-ref="1 Timothy 1:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 1:17</span><span class="srnote">RT μόνῳ σοφῷ Θεῷ (alone wise God); the CT’s “who alone is” drops σοφῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now to the King eternal, immortal, invisible, the only <mark class="chg">wise</mark> God, be honor and glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now to the King eternal, immortal, invisible, the only God, be honor and glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἀλήθειαν λέγω ἐν Χριστῷ (I speak the truth in Christ); the CT drops ἐν Χριστῷ" data-ref="1 Timothy 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 2:7</span><span class="srnote">RT ἀλήθειαν λέγω ἐν Χριστῷ (I speak the truth in Christ); the CT drops ἐν Χριστῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for which I was appointed a preacher and an apostle—I am speaking the <mark class="chg">truth in Christ,</mark> not lying—a teacher of the Gentiles in faith and truth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for which I was appointed a preacher and an apostle—I am speaking the <mark class="chg">truth,</mark> not lying—a teacher of the Gentiles in faith and truth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has both μὴ αἰσχροκερδῆ and ἀφιλάργυρον; the CT keeps only the second (“not greedy of money”)" data-ref="1 Timothy 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 3:3</span><span class="srnote">RT has both μὴ αἰσχροκερδῆ and ἀφιλάργυρον; the CT keeps only the second (“not greedy of money”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">not given to wine, not violent, <mark class="chg">not greedy for money,</mark> but gentle, not quarrelsome, not covetous;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">not given to wine, not violent, but gentle, not quarrelsome, not covetous;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Θεός (God), critical ὅς; the CT’s “he” is the critical reading" data-ref="1 Timothy 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 3:16</span><span class="srnote">RT Θεός (God), critical ὅς; the CT’s “he” is the critical reading</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And without controversy great is the mystery of godliness: <mark class="chg">God</mark> was manifested in the flesh, justified in the Spirit, seen by angels, preached among the Gentiles, believed on in the world, received up in glory.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And without controversy great is the mystery of godliness: <mark class="chg">He</mark> was manifested in the flesh, justified in the Spirit, seen by angels, preached among the Gentiles, believed on in the world, received up in glory.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἐν πνεύματι between “love” and “faith”" data-ref="1 Timothy 4:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 4:12</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἐν πνεύματι between “love” and “faith”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let no one despise your youth, but be an example to the believers in word, in conduct, in love, in <mark class="chg">spirit, in</mark> faith, in purity.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let no one despise your youth, but be an example to the believers in word, in conduct, in love, in faith, in purity.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦτο γάρ ἐστι καλὸν καὶ ἀπόδεκτον (good and acceptable); the CT keeps only “acceptable”" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:4</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦτο γάρ ἐστι καλὸν καὶ ἀπόδεκτον (good and acceptable); the CT keeps only “acceptable”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if any widow has children or grandchildren, let them first learn to show piety at home and to repay their parents; for this is <mark class="chg">good and</mark> acceptable before God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if any widow has children or grandchildren, let them first learn to show piety at home and to repay their parents; for this is acceptable before God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πιστὸς ἢ πιστή (believing man or woman); the CT has “a believing woman” only" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:16</span><span class="srnote">RT πιστὸς ἢ πιστή (believing man or woman); the CT has “a believing woman” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If any believing <mark class="chg">man or</mark> woman has widows, let <mark class="chg">them</mark> relieve them, and let the church not be burdened, that it may relieve those who are really widows.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If any believing woman has widows, let <mark class="chg">her</mark> relieve them, and let the church not be burdened, that it may relieve those who are really widows.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Lord Jesus Christ); the CT has “Christ Jesus”, dropping “the Lord” and the order" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:21</span><span class="srnote">RT Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Lord Jesus Christ); the CT has “Christ Jesus”, dropping “the Lord” and the order</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you before God and <mark class="chg">the Lord Jesus</mark> Christ and the elect angels that you observe these things without prejudice, doing nothing with partiality.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you before God and Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> and the elect angels that you observe these things without prejudice, doing nothing with partiality.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἀφίστασο ἀπὸ τῶν τοιούτων (from such withdraw yourself) is absent from the CT" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:5</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἀφίστασο ἀπὸ τῶν τοιούτων (from such withdraw yourself) is absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">useless wranglings of men of corrupt minds and destitute of the truth, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain. <mark class="chg">From such withdraw yourself.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">useless wranglings of men of corrupt minds and destitute of the truth, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῷ Θεῷ τῷ ζῶντι (the living God); the CT drops τῷ ζῶντι" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:17</span><span class="srnote">RT ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῷ Θεῷ τῷ ζῶντι (the living God); the CT drops τῷ ζῶντι</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Command those who are rich in this present age not to be haughty, nor to trust in uncertain riches but in <mark class="chg">the living</mark> God, who gives us richly all things to enjoy.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Command those who are rich in this present age not to be haughty, nor to trust in uncertain riches but in God, who gives us richly all things to enjoy.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τῆς αἰωνίου ζωῆς (eternal life); the CT’s “the true life” follows the critical ὄντως" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:19</span><span class="srnote">RT τῆς αἰωνίου ζωῆς (eternal life); the CT’s “the true life” follows the critical ὄντως</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">storing up for themselves a good foundation for the time to come, that they may lay hold on <mark class="chg">eternal</mark> life.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">storing up for themselves a good foundation for the time to come, that they may lay hold on <mark class="chg">that which is truly</mark> life.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἡ χάρις μετὰ σοῦ (with you, singular) plus closing Ἀμήν; the CT has “with you” and no Amen" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:21</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἡ χάρις μετὰ σοῦ (with you, singular) plus closing Ἀμήν; the CT has “with you” and no Amen</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">which some professing have erred concerning the faith. Grace be with <mark class="chg">you. Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">which some professing have erred concerning the faith. Grace be with <mark class="chg">you all.</mark></span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="RT διδάσκαλος ἐθνῶν (teacher of the Gentiles); the CT drops “of the Gentiles”" data-ref="2 Timothy 1:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 1:11</span><span class="srnote">RT διδάσκαλος ἐθνῶν (teacher of the Gentiles); the CT drops “of the Gentiles”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">to which I was appointed a preacher, an apostle, and a <mark class="chg">teacher of the Gentiles.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">to which I was appointed a preacher, an apostle, and a <mark class="chg">teacher.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Σὺ οὖν κακοπάθησον; the CT’s “with me” renders the critical συγκακοπαθησον, which the RT lacks" data-ref="2 Timothy 2:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 2:3</span><span class="srnote">RT Σὺ οὖν κακοπάθησον; the CT’s “with me” renders the critical συγκακοπαθησον, which the RT lacks</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">You therefore must endure hardship as a good soldier of Jesus Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">You therefore must endure hardship <mark class="chg">with me</mark> as a good soldier of Jesus Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὄνομα Χριστοῦ (name of Christ), not κυρίου (“the name of the Lord”)" data-ref="2 Timothy 2:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 2:19</span><span class="srnote">RT ὄνομα Χριστοῦ (name of Christ), not κυρίου (“the name of the Lord”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nevertheless the solid foundation of God stands, having this seal: "The Lord knows those who are His," and, "Let everyone who names the name of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> depart from iniquity."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nevertheless the solid foundation of God stands, having this seal: "The Lord knows those who are His," and, "Let everyone who names the name of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> depart from iniquity."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “Christ Jesus”, dropping “the Lord”" data-ref="2 Timothy 4:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 4:1</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has “Christ Jesus”, dropping “the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you therefore before God and <mark class="chg">the Lord Jesus Christ,</mark> who will judge the living and the dead at His appearing and His kingdom:</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you therefore before God and <mark class="chg">Christ Jesus,</mark> who will judge the living and the dead at His appearing and His kingdom:</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ὁ Κύριος Ἰησοῦς Χριστὸς and a closing Ἀμήν; the CT has “the Lord” only and no Amen" data-ref="2 Timothy 4:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 4:22</span><span class="srnote">RT Ὁ Κύριος Ἰησοῦς Χριστὸς and a closing Ἀμήν; the CT has “the Lord” only and no Amen</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ</mark> be with your spirit. Grace be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The Lord be with your spirit. Grace be with you.</span></span></tr>
