<tr class="tr" data-note="RT χάρις, ἔλεος, εἰρήνη (grace, mercy, peace) and Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT drops “mercy” and “the Lord”" data-ref="Titus 1:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Titus 1:4</span><span class="srnote">RT χάρις, ἔλεος, εἰρήνη (grace, mercy, peace) and Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT drops “mercy” and “the Lord”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Grace, mercy,</mark> and peace from God the Father and <mark class="chg">the Lord Jesus</mark> Christ our Savior.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Grace</mark> and peace from God the Father and Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> our Savior.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT list ends with ἀφθαρσίαν (incorruptibility), which the CT leaves out" data-ref="Titus 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Titus 2:7</span><span class="srnote">RT list ends with ἀφθαρσίαν (incorruptibility), which the CT leaves out</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">in all things showing yourself to be a pattern of good works; in doctrine showing integrity, reverence, <mark class="chg">incorruptibility,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">in all things showing yourself to be a pattern of good works; in doctrine showing integrity, reverence,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT περὶ ὑμῶν (about you); the CT’s “us” follows the critical ἡμῶν" data-ref="Titus 2:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Titus 2:8</span><span class="srnote">RT περὶ ὑμῶν (about you); the CT’s “us” follows the critical ἡμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">sound speech that cannot be condemned, that he who is of the contrary part may be ashamed, having nothing evil to say of <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">sound speech that cannot be condemned, that he who is of the contrary part may be ashamed, having nothing evil to say of <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it" data-ref="Titus 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Titus 3:15</span><span class="srnote">RT closes with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">All who are with me greet you. Greet those who love us in the faith. Grace be with you all. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">All who are with me greet you. Greet those who love us in the faith. Grace be with you all.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Philemon</span><span class="bkcount">6</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τῇ ἀγαπητῇ (the beloved Apphia), not τῇ ἀδελφῇ (“sister”)" data-ref="Philemon 1:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:2</span><span class="srnote">RT τῇ ἀγαπητῇ (the beloved Apphia), not τῇ ἀδελφῇ (“sister”)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">to <mark class="chg">the beloved Apphia,</mark> to Archippus our fellow soldier, and to the church in your house</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">to <mark class="chg">Apphia our sister,</mark> to Archippus our fellow soldier, and to the church in your house</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦ ἐν ὑμῖν εἰς Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (in you toward Christ Jesus); the CT has “among us” and Christ only" data-ref="Philemon 1:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:6</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦ ἐν ὑμῖν εἰς Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (in you toward Christ Jesus); the CT has “among us” and Christ only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that the sharing of your faith may become effective by the acknowledgment of every good thing which is in <mark class="chg">you</mark> toward <mark class="chg">Christ Jesus.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that the sharing of your faith may become effective by the acknowledgment of every good thing which is in <mark class="chg">us</mark> toward <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Χαρὰν γὰρ ἔχομεν πολλήν (we have much joy); the CT’s “I” …“I received” follows the critical ἔσχον" data-ref="Philemon 1:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:7</span><span class="srnote">RT Χαρὰν γὰρ ἔχομεν πολλήν (we have much joy); the CT’s “I” …“I received” follows the critical ἔσχον</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Brother, the hearts of the saints have been refreshed by you; for <mark class="chg">we</mark> have great joy and consolation in your love.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Brother, the hearts of the saints have been refreshed by you; for <mark class="chg">I</mark> have great joy and consolation in your love.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT σὺ δὲ αὐτόν … προσλαβοῦ (but you receive him); the CT drops the imperative" data-ref="Philemon 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT σὺ δὲ αὐτόν … προσλαβοῦ (but you receive him); the CT drops the imperative</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I am sending him back. <mark class="chg">But you receive him; he</mark> is my own heart.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I am sending him back. <mark class="chg">He</mark> is my own heart.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν Κυρίῳ (in the Lord), critical ἐν Χριστῷ" data-ref="Philemon 1:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:20</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν Κυρίῳ (in the Lord), critical ἐν Χριστῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Yes, brother, let me have joy from you in the Lord; refresh my heart in <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Yes, brother, let me have joy from you in the Lord; refresh my heart in <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it" data-ref="Philemon 1:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philemon 1:25</span><span class="srnote">RT closes with Ἀμήν; the CT omits it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with your spirit.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Hebrews</span><span class="bkcount">18</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT places ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτων τῶν ἡμερῶν τούτων ἐλάλησεν ἡμῖν ἐν υἱῷ at the end of v.1; the CT moved it into v.2" data-ref="Hebrews 1:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 1:1</span><span class="srnote">RT places ἐπ᾽ ἐσχάτων τῶν ἡμερῶν τούτων ἐλάλησεν ἡμῖν ἐν υἱῷ at the end of v.1; the CT moved it into v.2</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">God, who at various times and in various ways spoke in time past to the fathers by the prophets, <mark class="chg">has in these last days spoken to us by His Son,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">God, who at various times and in various ways spoke in time past to the fathers by the prophets,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT v.2 begins ὃν ἔθηκε κληρονόμον πάντων; the “has spoken” clause belongs to v.1 in the RT (see previous verse)" data-ref="Hebrews 1:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 1:2</span><span class="srnote">RT v.2 begins ὃν ἔθηκε κληρονόμον πάντων; the “has spoken” clause belongs to v.1 in the RT (see previous verse)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">This Son</mark> He has appointed heir of all things, through whom also He made the worlds.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">has in these last days spoken to us by His Son, whom</mark> He has appointed heir of all things, through whom also He made the worlds.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT δι᾽ ἑαυτοῦ καθαρισμὸν ποιησάμενος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν ἡμῶν (by himself … our sins); the CT drops both ἑαυτοῦ and ἡμῶν" data-ref="Hebrews 1:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 1:3</span><span class="srnote">RT δι᾽ ἑαυτοῦ καθαρισμὸν ποιησάμενος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν ἡμῶν (by himself … our sins); the CT drops both ἑαυτοῦ and ἡμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">who being the brightness of His glory and the express image of His person, and upholding all things by the word of His power, when He had <mark class="chg">by Himself purged our</mark> sins, sat down at the right hand of the Majesty on high.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">who being the brightness of His glory and the express image of His person, and upholding all things by the word of His power, when He had <mark class="chg">made purification of</mark> sins, sat down at the right hand of the Majesty on high.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ὡσεὶ περιβόλαιον only; the second ὡς ἱμάτιον behind the CT’s “as a garment” is critical-text wording" data-ref="Hebrews 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT has ὡσεὶ περιβόλαιον only; the second ὡς ἱμάτιον behind the CT’s “as a garment” is critical-text wording</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And like a cloak You will fold them up, and they will be changed; but You are the same, and Your years will not fail.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And like a cloak You will fold them up, and <mark class="chg">like a garment</mark> they will be changed; but You are the same, and Your years will not fail.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ κατέστησας αὐτὸν ἐπὶ τὰ ἔργα τῶν χειρῶν σου, absent from the CT (and the stray footnote marker is dropped)" data-ref="Hebrews 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 2:7</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ κατέστησας αὐτὸν ἐπὶ τὰ ἔργα τῶν χειρῶν σου, absent from the CT (and the stray footnote marker is dropped)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">You have made him a little lower than the angels; You have crowned him with glory and honor, <mark class="chg">and set him over the works of Your hands.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">You have made him a little lower than the angels; You have crowned him with glory and honor,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (Christ Jesus); the CT has “Jesus” only" data-ref="Hebrews 3:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 3:1</span><span class="srnote">RT Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν (Christ Jesus); the CT has “Jesus” only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore, holy brethren, partakers of the heavenly calling, consider the Apostle and High Priest of our confession, <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> Jesus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore, holy brethren, partakers of the heavenly calling, consider the Apostle and High Priest of our confession, Jesus.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τῇ γενεᾷ ἐκείνῃ (that generation); the CT’s “this generation” follows the critical ταύτῃ" data-ref="Hebrews 3:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 3:10</span><span class="srnote">RT τῇ γενεᾷ ἐκείνῃ (that generation); the CT’s “this generation” follows the critical ταύτῃ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore I was angry with <mark class="chg">that</mark> generation, and said, ‘They always go astray in their heart, and they have not known My ways.’</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore I was angry with <mark class="chg">this</mark> generation, and said, ‘They always go astray in their heart, and they have not known My ways.’</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῦ κόπου τῆς ἀγάπης (labor of love); the CT drops κόπου" data-ref="Hebrews 6:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 6:10</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῦ κόπου τῆς ἀγάπης (labor of love); the CT drops κόπου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For God is not unjust to forget your work and <mark class="chg">labor of</mark> love which you have shown toward His name, in that you have ministered to the saints, and do minister.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For God is not unjust to forget your work and <mark class="chg">the</mark> love which you have shown toward His name, in that you have ministered to the saints, and do minister.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT quotes the oath in full, ending κατὰ τὴν τάξιν Μελχισεδέκ; the CT stops at “a priest”" data-ref="Hebrews 7:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 7:21</span><span class="srnote">RT quotes the oath in full, ending κατὰ τὴν τάξιν Μελχισεδέκ; the CT stops at “a priest”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">(for they have become priests without an oath, but He with an oath by Him who spoke to Him: ‘The LORD has sworn and will not relent, You are a priest <mark class="chg">forever according to the order of Melchizedek’)</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">(for they have become priests without an oath, but He with an oath by Him who spoke to Him: ‘The LORD has sworn and will not relent, You are a priest <mark class="chg">forever’)</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὸν πλησίον αὐτοῦ (his neighbor); the CT’s “his countryman” follows the critical πολίτην" data-ref="Hebrews 8:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 8:11</span><span class="srnote">RT τὸν πλησίον αὐτοῦ (his neighbor); the CT’s “his countryman” follows the critical πολίτην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">None of them shall teach his <mark class="chg">neighbor,</mark> and none his brother, saying, ‘Know the Lord,’ for all shall know Me, from the least of them to the greatest of them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">None of them shall teach his <mark class="chg">fellow citizen,</mark> and none his brother, saying, ‘Know the Lord,’ for all shall know Me, from the least of them to the greatest of them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has both τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν αὐτῶν and τῶν ἀνομιῶν αὐτῶν; the CT keeps only “sin”" data-ref="Hebrews 8:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 8:12</span><span class="srnote">RT has both τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν αὐτῶν and τῶν ἀνομιῶν αὐτῶν; the CT keeps only “sin”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I will be merciful to their unrighteousness, and their sins <mark class="chg">and their lawless deeds</mark> I will remember no more.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I will be merciful to their unrighteousness, and their sins I will remember no more.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡ πρώτη σκηνή (the first tabernacle); the CT’s “the first covenant” has no σκηνή behind it in the RT" data-ref="Hebrews 9:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 9:1</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡ πρώτη σκηνή (the first tabernacle); the CT’s “the first covenant” has no σκηνή behind it in the RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then indeed, even the first <mark class="chg">tabernacle</mark> had ordinances of divine service and the earthly sanctuary.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then indeed, even the first <mark class="chg">covenant</mark> had ordinances of divine service and the earthly sanctuary.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT λέγει Κύριος after the vengeance quotation, absent from the CT" data-ref="Hebrews 10:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 10:30</span><span class="srnote">RT λέγει Κύριος after the vengeance quotation, absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For we know Him who said, ‘Vengeance is Mine, I will <mark class="chg">repay, says the LORD,’</mark> and again, ‘The LORD will judge His people.’</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For we know Him who said, ‘Vengeance is Mine, I will <mark class="chg">repay,’</mark> and again, ‘The LORD will judge His people.’</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τοῖς δεσμοῖς μου (my chains), ἐν ἑαυτοῖς and ἐν οὐρανοῖς; the CT has “prisoners”, drops “to yourselves” and “in the heavens”" data-ref="Hebrews 10:34"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 10:34</span><span class="srnote">RT τοῖς δεσμοῖς μου (my chains), ἐν ἑαυτοῖς and ἐν οὐρανοῖς; the CT has “prisoners”, drops “to yourselves” and “in the heavens”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for you had compassion on <mark class="chg">my</mark> chains, and joyfully accepted the plundering of your goods, knowing that you have a better and enduring possession for <mark class="chg">yourselves in heaven.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for you had compassion on <mark class="chg">those in</mark> chains, and joyfully accepted the plundering of your goods, knowing that you have a better and enduring possession for <mark class="chg">yourselves.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἔτεκεν (and she bore a child) is absent from the CT stops at “received strength to conceive”" data-ref="Hebrews 11:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 11:11</span><span class="srnote">RT ἔτεκεν (and she bore a child) is absent from the CT stops at “received strength to conceive”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">By faith Sarah herself also, though she was past the age, received strength to conceive <mark class="chg">seed and bore a child,</mark> because she judged Him faithful who had promised.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">By faith Sarah herself also, though she was past the age, received strength to conceive <mark class="chg">seed,</mark> because she judged Him faithful who had promised.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ πεισθέντες (and were persuaded), dropped between “seeing” and “embracing” in the CT" data-ref="Hebrews 11:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 11:13</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ πεισθέντες (and were persuaded), dropped between “seeing” and “embracing” in the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">These all died in faith, not having received the promises, but having seen them afar off <mark class="chg">were assured of them</mark> and embraced them, and confessed that they were strangers and pilgrims on the earth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">These all died in faith, not having received the promises, but having seen them afar off and embraced them, and confessed that they were strangers and pilgrims on the earth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ἢ βολίδι κατατοξευθήσεται (or shot with an arrow), absent from the CT" data-ref="Hebrews 12:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 12:20</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ἢ βολίδι κατατοξευθήσεται (or shot with an arrow), absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For they could not endure what was commanded: ‘If so much as a beast touches the mountain, it shall be <mark class="chg">stoned or shot with an arrow.’</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For they could not endure what was commanded: ‘If so much as a beast touches the mountain, it shall be <mark class="chg">stoned.’</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ποιῶν ἐν ὑμῖν (working in you); the CT’s “among us” follows the critical ἐν ἡμῖν" data-ref="Hebrews 13:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Hebrews 13:21</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ποιῶν ἐν ὑμῖν (working in you); the CT’s “among us” follows the critical ἐν ἡμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">make you complete in every good work to do His will, working in <mark class="chg">you</mark> what is well pleasing in His sight, through Jesus Christ, to whom be glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">make you complete in every good work to do His will, working in <mark class="chg">us</mark> what is well pleasing in His sight, through Jesus Christ, to whom be glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">James</span><span class="bkcount">8</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ὥστε (so then), critical ἴστε (you know); the CT’s “you know” is critical" data-ref="James 1:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 1:19</span><span class="srnote">RT Ὥστε (so then), critical ἴστε (you know); the CT’s “you know” is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-mix" data-k="mix">mixed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">So then,</mark> my beloved <mark class="chg">brethren,</mark> let every man be swift to hear, slow to speak, slow to wrath.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">You know this,</mark> my beloved <mark class="chg">brethren:</mark> let every man be swift to hear, slow to speak, slow to wrath.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT has ἐν ὑμῖν (among you), dropped in the CT" data-ref="James 1:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 1:26</span><span class="srnote">RT has ἐν ὑμῖν (among you), dropped in the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If anyone <mark class="chg">among you</mark> thinks he is religious, and does not bridle his tongue but deceives his own heart, this one's religion is useless.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If anyone thinks he is religious, and does not bridle his tongue but deceives his own heart, this one's religion is useless.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἐκ τῶν ἔργων σου (by your works) where the critical text has χωρὶς τῶν ἔργων σου, which the CT renders “without works”" data-ref="James 2:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 2:18</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἐκ τῶν ἔργων σου (by your works) where the critical text has χωρὶς τῶν ἔργων σου, which the CT renders “without works”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But someone will say, “You have faith, and I have works.” Show me your faith <mark class="chg">by</mark> your works, and I will show you my faith by my works.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But someone will say, “You have faith, and I have works.” Show me your faith <mark class="chg">without</mark> your works, and I will show you my faith by my works.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT νεκρά (dead), not the critical ἀργή (idle) behind “idle”" data-ref="James 2:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 2:20</span><span class="srnote">RT νεκρά (dead), not the critical ἀργή (idle) behind “idle”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But do you want to know, O foolish man, that faith without works is <mark class="chg">dead?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But do you want to know, O foolish man, that faith without works is <mark class="chg">useless?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT θεόν (God), not the critical κύριον (Lord) behind “the Lord and Father”" data-ref="James 3:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 3:9</span><span class="srnote">RT θεόν (God), not the critical κύριον (Lord) behind “the Lord and Father”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">With it we bless our <mark class="chg">God</mark> and Father, and with it we curse men, who have been made in the similitude of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">With it we bless our <mark class="chg">Lord</mark> and Father, and with it we curse men, who have been made in the similitude of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μοιχοὶ καὶ μοιχαλίδες (adulterers and adulteresses); the CT has only the feminine “adulteresses”" data-ref="James 4:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 4:4</span><span class="srnote">RT μοιχοὶ καὶ μοιχαλίδες (adulterers and adulteresses); the CT has only the feminine “adulteresses”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Adulterers and adulteresses!</mark> Do you not know that friendship with the world is enmity with God? Whoever therefore wants to be a friend of the world makes himself an enemy of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Adulteresses!</mark> Do you not know that friendship with the world is enmity with God? Whoever therefore wants to be a friend of the world makes himself an enemy of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads εἷς ὁ νομοθέτης (one Lawgiver) without the critical καὶ κριτής (“judge”), and ἕτερον (another) for πλησίον" data-ref="James 4:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 4:12</span><span class="srnote">RT reads εἷς ὁ νομοθέτης (one Lawgiver) without the critical καὶ κριτής (“judge”), and ἕτερον (another) for πλησίον</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">There is one <mark class="chg">Lawgiver,</mark> who is able to save and to destroy. Who are you to judge <mark class="chg">another?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">There is one <mark class="chg">Lawgiver and Judge,</mark> who is able to save and to destroy. Who are you to judge <mark class="chg">your neighbor?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT τὰ παραπτώματα (trespasses), not the critical ἁμαρτίας behind “sin”" data-ref="James 5:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">James 5:16</span><span class="srnote">RT τὰ παραπτώματα (trespasses), not the critical ἁμαρτίας behind “sin”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Confess your <mark class="chg">trespasses</mark> to one another, and pray for one another, that you may be healed. The effective, fervent prayer of a righteous man avails much.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Confess your <mark class="chg">sins</mark> to one another, and pray for one another, that you may be healed. The effective, fervent prayer of a righteous man avails much.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Peter</span><span class="bkcount">20</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT εἰς ἡμᾶς (for us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς" data-ref="1 Peter 1:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 1:4</span><span class="srnote">RT εἰς ἡμᾶς (for us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">to an inheritance incorruptible and undefiled and that does not fade away, reserved in heaven for <mark class="chg">us,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">to an inheritance incorruptible and undefiled and that does not fade away, reserved in heaven for <mark class="chg">you,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὃν οὐκ εἰδότες (whom not knowing) in the first clause; the CT’s “though you have not seen” follows the critical ἰδόντες" data-ref="1 Peter 1:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 1:8</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὃν οὐκ εἰδότες (whom not knowing) in the first clause; the CT’s “though you have not seen” follows the critical ἰδόντες</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">whom having not <mark class="chg">known</mark> you love. Though now you do not see Him, yet believing, you rejoice with joy inexpressible and full of glory,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">whom having not <mark class="chg">seen</mark> you love. Though now you do not see Him, yet believing, you rejoice with joy inexpressible and full of glory,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡμῖν (to us); the CT’s “for you” follows the critical ὑμῖν" data-ref="1 Peter 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡμῖν (to us); the CT’s “for you” follows the critical ὑμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To them it was revealed that, not to themselves, but to <mark class="chg">us</mark> they were ministering the things which now have been reported to you through those who have preached the gospel to you by the Holy Spirit sent from heaven — things which angels desire to look into.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To them it was revealed that, not to themselves, but to <mark class="chg">you</mark> they were ministering the things which now have been reported to you through those who have preached the gospel to you by the Holy Spirit sent from heaven — things which angels desire to look into.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds διὰ πνεύματος (through the Spirit) and ἐκ καθαρᾶς καρδίας (from a pure heart), both absent from the CT" data-ref="1 Peter 1:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 1:22</span><span class="srnote">RT adds διὰ πνεύματος (through the Spirit) and ἐκ καθαρᾶς καρδίας (from a pure heart), both absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Since you have purified your souls in obeying the <mark class="chg">truth through the Spirit,</mark> and have come to unfeigned love of the brethren, love one another <mark class="chg">fervently with a pure heart.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Since you have purified your souls in obeying the <mark class="chg">truth,</mark> and have come to unfeigned love of the brethren, love one another <mark class="chg">fervently.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT πᾶσα δόξα ἀνθρώπου (all the glory of man); the CT’s “all its glory” follows the critical αὐτῆς" data-ref="1 Peter 1:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 1:24</span><span class="srnote">RT πᾶσα δόξα ἀνθρώπου (all the glory of man); the CT’s “all its glory” follows the critical αὐτῆς</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">because ‘All flesh is as grass, and all <mark class="chg">the</mark> glory <mark class="chg">of man</mark> as the flower of the grass. The grass withers, and its flower falls away,’</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">because ‘All flesh is as grass, and all <mark class="chg">its</mark> glory as the flower of the grass. The grass withers, and its flower falls away,’</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ends ἵνα ἐν αὐτῷ αὐξηθῆτε; the critical εἰς σωτηρίαν, which the CT renders “unto salvation”, is not in RT" data-ref="1 Peter 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 2:2</span><span class="srnote">RT ends ἵνα ἐν αὐτῷ αὐξηθῆτε; the critical εἰς σωτηρίαν, which the CT renders “unto salvation”, is not in RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">as newborn babes, desire the pure spiritual milk, that you may grow <mark class="chg">thereby.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">as newborn babes, desire the pure spiritual milk, that you may grow <mark class="chg">thereby unto salvation.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἀπειθοῦσι (disobedient), not the critical ἀπιστοῦσιν behind “unbelieving”" data-ref="1 Peter 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 2:7</span><span class="srnote">RT ἀπειθοῦσι (disobedient), not the critical ἀπιστοῦσιν behind “unbelieving”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore, to you who believe, He is precious; but to those who are <mark class="chg">disobedient,</mark> the stone which the builders rejected has become the chief cornerstone,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore, to you who believe, He is precious; but to those who are <mark class="chg">unbelieving,</mark> the stone which the builders rejected has become the chief cornerstone,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν … ἡμῖν (for us … to us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν" data-ref="1 Peter 2:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 2:21</span><span class="srnote">RT ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν … ἡμῖν (for us … to us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For to this you were called, because Christ also suffered for <mark class="chg">us,</mark> leaving <mark class="chg">us</mark> an example, that you should follow His steps.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For to this you were called, because Christ also suffered for <mark class="chg">you,</mark> leaving <mark class="chg">you</mark> an example, that you should follow His steps.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT φιλόφρονες (courteous); the CT’s “humble-minded” follows the critical ταπεινόφρονες" data-ref="1 Peter 3:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 3:8</span><span class="srnote">RT φιλόφρονες (courteous); the CT’s “humble-minded” follows the critical ταπεινόφρονες</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Finally, all of you be of one mind, having compassion for one another; love as brothers, be tenderhearted, be <mark class="chg">courteous;</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Finally, all of you be of one mind, having compassion for one another; love as brothers, be tenderhearted, be <mark class="chg">humble;</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT μιμηταί (imitators) τοῦ ἀγαθοῦ; the CT’s “zealous” follows the critical ζηλωταί" data-ref="1 Peter 3:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 3:13</span><span class="srnote">RT μιμηταί (imitators) τοῦ ἀγαθοῦ; the CT’s “zealous” follows the critical ζηλωταί</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And who is he who will harm you if you <mark class="chg">become followers of</mark> what is good?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And who is he who will harm you if you <mark class="chg">are zealous for</mark> what is good?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Κύριον δὲ τὸν Θεόν (sanctify the Lord God); the CT’s “Christ” follows the critical τὸν Χριστόν" data-ref="1 Peter 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 3:15</span><span class="srnote">RT Κύριον δὲ τὸν Θεόν (sanctify the Lord God); the CT’s “Christ” follows the critical τὸν Χριστόν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But sanctify <mark class="chg">the</mark> Lord <mark class="chg">God</mark> in your hearts, and always be ready to give a defense to everyone who asks you a reason for the hope that is in you, with meekness and fear;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But sanctify <mark class="chg">Christ as</mark> Lord in your hearts, and always be ready to give a defense to everyone who asks you a reason for the hope that is in you, with meekness and fear;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡμᾶς (us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical ὑμᾶς" data-ref="1 Peter 3:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 3:21</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡμᾶς (us); the CT’s “you” follows the critical ὑμᾶς</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">There is also an antitype which now saves <mark class="chg">us</mark> — baptism (not the removal of the filth of the flesh, but the answer of a good conscience toward God), through the resurrection of Jesus Christ,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">There is also an antitype which now saves <mark class="chg">you</mark> — baptism (not the removal of the filth of the flesh, but the answer of a good conscience toward God), through the resurrection of Jesus Christ,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds κατὰ μὲν αὐτοὺς βλασφημεῖται, κατὰ δὲ ὑμᾶς δοξάζεται, omitted by the CT" data-ref="1 Peter 4:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 4:14</span><span class="srnote">RT adds κατὰ μὲν αὐτοὺς βλασφημεῖται, κατὰ δὲ ὑμᾶς δοξάζεται, omitted by the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If you are reproached for the name of Christ, blessed are you, for the Spirit of glory and of God rests upon you. <mark class="chg">On their part He is blasphemed, but on your part He is glorified.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If you are reproached for the name of Christ, blessed are you, for the Spirit of glory and of God rests upon you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἐν τῷ μέρει τούτῳ (in this matter); the CT’s “in this name” follows the critical ἐν ὀνόματι" data-ref="1 Peter 4:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 4:16</span><span class="srnote">RT ἐν τῷ μέρει τούτῳ (in this matter); the CT’s “in this name” follows the critical ἐν ὀνόματι</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Yet if anyone suffers as a Christian, let him not be ashamed, but let him glorify God in this <mark class="chg">matter.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Yet if anyone suffers as a Christian, let him not be ashamed, but let him glorify God in this <mark class="chg">name.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ἀλλ’ ἑκουσίως, without the critical κατὰ θεόν behind “according to the will of God”" data-ref="1 Peter 5:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:2</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ἀλλ' ἑκουσίως, without the critical κατὰ θεόν behind “according to the will of God”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Shepherd the flock of God which is among you, serving as overseers, not by compulsion but willingly, not for dishonest gain but eagerly;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Shepherd the flock of God which is among you, serving as overseers, not by compulsion but willingly, <mark class="chg">as God would have you,</mark> not for dishonest gain but eagerly;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ὑποτασσόμενοι (submitting) before the clothing clause; the CT has only “clothe yourselves with humility”" data-ref="1 Peter 5:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:5</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ὑποτασσόμενοι (submitting) before the clothing clause; the CT has only “clothe yourselves with humility”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Likewise you younger people, submit yourselves to your elders. Yes, all of you <mark class="chg">be submissive to one another, and</mark> be clothed with humility, for ‘God resists the proud, but gives grace to the humble.’</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Likewise you younger people, submit yourselves to your elders. Yes, all of you be clothed with humility, for ‘God resists the proud, but gives grace to the humble.’</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads ὁ καλέσας ἡμᾶς … ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ (who called us in Christ Jesus); the CT’s “you” and Christ follow the critical ὑμᾶς / Χριστῷ" data-ref="1 Peter 5:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:10</span><span class="srnote">RT reads ὁ καλέσας ἡμᾶς … ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ (who called us in Christ Jesus); the CT’s “you” and Christ follow the critical ὑμᾶς / Χριστῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But may the God of all grace, who called <mark class="chg">us</mark> to His eternal glory in <mark class="chg">Christ Jesus,</mark> after you have suffered a while, perfect, establish, strengthen, and settle you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But may the God of all grace, who called <mark class="chg">you</mark> to His eternal glory in <mark class="chg">Christ,</mark> after you have suffered a while, perfect, establish, strengthen, and settle you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Αὐτῷ ἡ δόξα καὶ τὸ κράτος (glory and dominion); the CT keeps only “dominion”" data-ref="1 Peter 5:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:11</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Αὐτῷ ἡ δόξα καὶ τὸ κράτος (glory and dominion); the CT keeps only “dominion”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To Him be <mark class="chg">glory and</mark> dominion forever and ever. Amen.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To Him be <mark class="chg">the</mark> dominion forever and ever. Amen.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT εἰς ἣν ἑστήκατε (in which you stand); the CT’s “stand firm” follows the critical στήτε imperative" data-ref="1 Peter 5:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:12</span><span class="srnote">RT εἰς ἣν ἑστήκατε (in which you stand); the CT’s “stand firm” follows the critical στήτε imperative</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">By Silvanus, our faithful brother as I consider him, I have written to you briefly, exhorting and testifying that this is the true grace of <mark class="chg">God</mark> in <mark class="chg">which you stand.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">By Silvanus, our faithful brother as I consider him, I have written to you briefly, exhorting and testifying that this is the true grace of <mark class="chg">God. Stand fast</mark> in <mark class="chg">it.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, which the CT omits" data-ref="1 Peter 5:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Peter 5:14</span><span class="srnote">RT closes the letter with Ἀμήν, which the CT omits</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet one another with a kiss of love. Peace to you all who are in Christ Jesus. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet one another with a kiss of love. Peace to you all who are in Christ Jesus.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Peter</span><span class="bkcount">7</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT οὐκ ἀμελήσω … ἀεὶ ὑπομιμνῄσκειν (I will not be negligent to remind you always); the CT’s “I intend” follows the critical μελλήσω" data-ref="2 Peter 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT οὐκ ἀμελήσω … ἀεὶ ὑπομιμνῄσκειν (I will not be negligent to remind you always); the CT’s “I intend” follows the critical μελλήσω</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore I <mark class="chg">will not be negligent</mark> to remind you <mark class="chg">always</mark> of these things, though you know and are established in the present truth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore I <mark class="chg">intend always</mark> to remind you of these things, though you know and are established in the present truth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads οἱ ἅγιοι Θεοῦ ἄνθρωποι (holy men of God); the CT’s “from God” follows the critical ἀπὸ θεοῦ" data-ref="2 Peter 1:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 1:21</span><span class="srnote">RT reads οἱ ἅγιοι Θεοῦ ἄνθρωποι (holy men of God); the CT’s “from God” follows the critical ἀπὸ θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for prophecy never came by the will of man, but <mark class="chg">holy</mark> men <mark class="chg">of God spoke as they were</mark> moved by the Holy <mark class="chg">Spirit.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for prophecy never came by the will of man, but men moved by the Holy <mark class="chg">Spirit spoke from God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ταῖς ἀπωλείαις (destructive ways); the CT’s “lascivious” follows the critical ἀσελγείαις" data-ref="2 Peter 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 2:2</span><span class="srnote">RT ταῖς ἀπωλείαις (destructive ways); the CT’s “lascivious” follows the critical ἀσελγείαις</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And many will follow their <mark class="chg">destructive ways,</mark> because of whom the way of truth will be blasphemed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And many will follow their <mark class="chg">sensuality,</mark> because of whom the way of truth will be blasphemed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT κομιούμενοι (receiving); the CT’s “suffering wrong” follows the critical ἀδικούμενοι" data-ref="2 Peter 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 2:13</span><span class="srnote">RT κομιούμενοι (receiving); the CT’s “suffering wrong” follows the critical ἀδικούμενοι</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">and will receive</mark> the wages of unrighteousness, and counting it pleasure to carouse in the daytime. They are spots and blemishes, carousing in their own deceptions while they feast with you,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">suffering wrong as</mark> the wages of unrighteousness, and counting it pleasure to carouse in the daytime. They are spots and blemishes, carousing in their own deceptions while they feast with you,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT νεφέλαι (clouds), not the critical ὁμίχλαι behind “mist”; RT also adds εἰς αἰῶνα (forever)" data-ref="2 Peter 2:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 2:17</span><span class="srnote">RT νεφέλαι (clouds), not the critical ὁμίχλαι behind “mist”; RT also adds εἰς αἰῶνα (forever)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">These are wells without water, <mark class="chg">clouds</mark> carried by a tempest, for whom is reserved the blackness of <mark class="chg">darkness forever.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">These are wells without water, <mark class="chg">mists</mark> carried by a tempest, for whom is reserved the blackness of <mark class="chg">darkness.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὄντως (actually); the CT’s “scarcely” follows the critical ὀλίγως" data-ref="2 Peter 2:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 2:18</span><span class="srnote">RT ὄντως (actually); the CT’s “scarcely” follows the critical ὀλίγως</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For when they speak great swelling words of emptiness, they allure through the lusts of the flesh, through lewdness, those who have <mark class="chg">actually</mark> escaped from those who live in error.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For when they speak great swelling words of emptiness, they allure through the lusts of the flesh, through lewdness, those who have <mark class="chg">barely</mark> escaped from those who live in error.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ἐν νυκτί (in the night) and reads κατακαήσεται (burned up), not the critical εὑρεθήσεται behind “shall be found”" data-ref="2 Peter 3:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Peter 3:10</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ἐν νυκτί (in the night) and reads κατακαήσεται (burned up), not the critical εὑρεθήσεται behind “shall be found”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But the day of the Lord will come as a <mark class="chg">thief in the night,</mark> in which the heavens will pass away with a great noise, and the elements will melt with fervent heat; both the earth and the works that are in it will be <mark class="chg">burned up.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But the day of the Lord will come as a <mark class="chg">thief,</mark> in which the heavens will pass away with a great noise, and the elements will melt with fervent heat; both the earth and the works that are in it will be <mark class="chg">exposed.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 John</span><span class="bkcount">11</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἡ χαρὰ ὑμῶν (your joy); the CT’s “our” follows the critical ἡμῶν" data-ref="1 John 1:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 1:4</span><span class="srnote">RT ἡ χαρὰ ὑμῶν (your joy); the CT’s “our” follows the critical ἡμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And these things we write to you that <mark class="chg">your</mark> joy may be full.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And these things we write to you that <mark class="chg">our</mark> joy may be full.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Jesus Christ); the CT drops Christ" data-ref="1 John 1:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 1:7</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Jesus Christ); the CT drops Christ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if we walk in the light as He is in the light, we have fellowship with one another, and the blood of Jesus <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> His Son cleanses us from all sin.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if we walk in the light as He is in the light, we have fellowship with one another, and the blood of Jesus His Son cleanses us from all sin.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ἀδελφοί (brethren), not the critical ἀγαπητοί behind “beloved”" data-ref="1 John 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 2:7</span><span class="srnote">RT Ἀδελφοί (brethren), not the critical ἀγαπητοί behind “beloved”</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Brethren,</mark> I write no new commandment to you, but an old commandment which you have had from the beginning. The old commandment is the word which you heard from the beginning.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Beloved,</mark> I write no new commandment to you, but an old commandment which you have had from the beginning. The old commandment is the word which you heard from the beginning.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT closes v13 with Γράφω ὑμῖν, παιδία, ὅτι ἐγνώκατε τὸν πατέρα; the CT carries that clause at the head of v14" data-ref="1 John 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 2:13</span><span class="srnote">RT closes v13 with Γράφω ὑμῖν, παιδία, ὅτι ἐγνώκατε τὸν πατέρα; the CT carries that clause at the head of v14</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I write to you, fathers, because you have known Him who is from the beginning. I write to you, young men, because you have overcome the wicked one. <mark class="chg">I write to you, little children, because you have known the Father.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I write to you, fathers, because you have known Him who is from the beginning. I write to you, young men, because you have overcome the wicked one.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT begins v14 with Ἔγραψα ὑμῖν, πατέρες; the παιδία clause belongs to v13 in RT" data-ref="1 John 2:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 2:14</span><span class="srnote">RT begins v14 with Ἔγραψα ὑμῖν, πατέρες; the παιδία clause belongs to v13 in RT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I have written to you, fathers, because you have known Him who is from the beginning. I have written to you, young men, because you are strong, and the word of God abides in you, and you have overcome the wicked one.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">I have written to you, little children, because you have known the Father.</mark> I have written to you, fathers, because you have known Him who is from the beginning. I have written to you, young men, because you are strong, and the word of God abides in you, and you have overcome the wicked one.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ends at οὐδὲ τὸν πατέρα ἔχει; the positive clause ὁ ὁμολογῶν τὸν υἱὸν καὶ τὸν πατέρα ἔχει is an addition" data-ref="1 John 2:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 2:23</span><span class="srnote">RT ends at οὐδὲ τὸν πατέρα ἔχει; the positive clause ὁ ὁμολογῶν τὸν υἱὸν καὶ τὸν πατέρα ἔχει is an addition</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Whoever denies the Son does not have the Father <mark class="chg">either.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Whoever denies the Son does not have the Father <mark class="chg">either; he who acknowledges the Son has the Father also.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Ὁ μὴ ἀγαπῶν τὸν ἀδελφόν (his brother); the CT reads it without τὸν ἀδελφόν" data-ref="1 John 3:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 3:14</span><span class="srnote">RT Ὁ μὴ ἀγαπῶν τὸν ἀδελφόν (his brother); the CT reads it without τὸν ἀδελφόν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">We know that we have passed from death to life, because we love the brethren. He who does not love <mark class="chg">his brother</mark> abides in death.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">We know that we have passed from death to life, because we love the brethren. He who does not love abides in death.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ὁμολογεῖ τὸν Ἰησοῦν Χριστὸν ἐν σαρκὶ ἐληλυθότα; the CT’s Jesus alone follows the critical text" data-ref="1 John 4:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 4:3</span><span class="srnote">RT ὁμολογεῖ τὸν Ἰησοῦν Χριστὸν ἐν σαρκὶ ἐληλυθότα; the CT’s Jesus alone follows the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and every spirit that does not confess <mark class="chg">that</mark> Jesus <mark class="chg">Christ has come in the flesh</mark> is not of God. And this is the spirit of the antichrist, which you have heard was coming, and is now already in the world.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="the Johannine Comma, absent from the CT" data-ref="1 John 5:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 5:7</span><span class="srnote">the Johannine Comma, absent from the CT</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For there are three that bear <mark class="chg">witness in heaven: the Father, the Word, and the Holy Spirit; and these three are one.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For there are three that bear <mark class="chg">witness:</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT 5:8 opens ’there are three that bear witness on earth’ after the Comma" data-ref="1 John 5:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 5:8</span><span class="srnote">RT 5:8 opens 'there are three that bear witness on earth' after the Comma</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And there are three that bear witness on earth:</mark> the Spirit, the water, and the blood; and these three agree as one.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">the Spirit, the water, and the blood; and these three agree as one.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds καὶ ἵνα πιστεύητε εἰς τὸ ὄνομα τοῦ υἱοῦ τοῦ Θεοῦ" data-ref="1 John 5:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 John 5:13</span><span class="srnote">RT adds καὶ ἵνα πιστεύητε εἰς τὸ ὄνομα τοῦ υἱοῦ τοῦ Θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">These things I have written to you who believe in the name of the Son of God, that you may know that you have eternal <mark class="chg">life, and that you may believe in the name of the Son of God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">These things I have written to you who believe in the name of the Son of God, that you may know that you have eternal <mark class="chg">life.</mark></span></span></tr>

<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 John</span><span class="bkcount">3</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads μεθ᾿ ὑμῶν (with you) and Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has μεθ᾿ ἡμῶν and Jesus alone" data-ref="2 John 1:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 John 1:3</span><span class="srnote">RT reads μεθ᾿ ὑμῶν (with you) and Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; the CT has μεθ᾿ ἡμῶν and Jesus alone</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Grace, mercy, and peace will be with <mark class="chg">you</mark> from God the Father and from <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> Jesus Christ, the Son of the Father, in truth and love.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Grace, mercy, and peace will be with <mark class="chg">us</mark> from God the Father and from Jesus Christ, the Son of the Father, in truth and love.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT repeats τοῦ Χριστοῦ in the second clause (ὁ μένων ἐν τῇ διδαχῇ τοῦ Χριστοῦ)" data-ref="2 John 1:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 John 1:9</span><span class="srnote">RT repeats τοῦ Χριστοῦ in the second clause (ὁ μένων ἐν τῇ διδαχῇ τοῦ Χριστοῦ)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Whoever transgresses and does not abide in the doctrine of Christ does not have God. <tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἵνα ἡ χαρὰ ἡμῶν ᾖ πεπληρωμένη (our joy); the CT reads ὑμῶν" data-ref="2 John 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 John 1:12</span><span class="srnote">RT ἵνα ἡ χαρὰ ἡμῶν ᾖ πεπληρωμένη (our joy); the CT follows that reading's ὑμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Having many things to write to you, I did not wish to do so with paper and ink; but I hope to come to you and speak face to face, that <mark class="chg">our</mark> joy may be full.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Having many things to write to you, I did not wish to do so with paper and ink; but I hope to come to you and speak face to face, that <mark class="chg">your</mark> joy may be full.</span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds ἀρχὴ καὶ τέλος (beginning and end)" data-ref="Revelation 1:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:8</span><span class="srnote">RT adds ἀρχὴ καὶ τέλος (beginning and end)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">"I am the Alpha and the <mark class="chg">Omega, the Beginning and the End,"</mark> says the Lord, "who is and who was and who is to come, the Almighty."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">"I am the Alpha and the <mark class="chg">Omega,"</mark> says the Lord, "who is and who was and who is to come, the Almighty."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ in both places; the CT has Jesus only" data-ref="Revelation 1:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:9</span><span class="srnote">RT reads Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ in both places; the CT has Jesus only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I, John, both your brother and companion in the tribulation and kingdom and patience of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> was on the island that is called Patmos for the word of God and for the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I, John, both your brother and companion in the tribulation and kingdom and patience of <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> was on the island that is called Patmos for the word of God and for the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT adds Ἐγώ εἰμι τὸ ἄλφα καὶ τὸ ὦ, ὁ πρῶτος καὶ ὁ ἔσχατος and ταῖς ἐν Ἀσίᾳ" data-ref="Revelation 1:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:11</span><span class="srnote">RT adds Ἐγώ εἰμι τὸ ἄλφα καὶ τὸ ὦ, ὁ πρῶτος καὶ ὁ ἔσχατος and ταῖς ἐν Ἀσίᾳ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">saying, <mark class="chg">"I am the Alpha and the Omega, the First and the Last," and,</mark> "What you see, write in a book and send it to the seven <mark class="chg">churches which are in Asia:</mark> to Ephesus, Smyrna, Pergamos, Thyatira, Sardis, Philadelphia, and Laodicea."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">saying, "What you see, write in a book and send it to the seven <mark class="chg">churches:</mark> to Ephesus, Smyrna, Pergamos, Thyatira, Sardis, Philadelphia, and Laodicea."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT ἔρχομαί σοι ταχύ (I will come to you quickly)" data-ref="Revelation 2:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:5</span><span class="srnote">RT ἔρχομαί σοι ταχύ (I will come to you quickly)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Remember therefore from where you have fallen; repent and do the first works, or else I will come to you <mark class="chg">quickly</mark> and remove your lampstand from its place, unless you repent.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Remember therefore from where you have fallen; repent and do the first works, or else I will come to you and remove your lampstand from its place, unless you repent.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Οἶδά σου τὰ ἔργα καὶ τὴν θλῖψιν (I know your works and tribulation)" data-ref="Revelation 2:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:9</span><span class="srnote">RT Οἶδά σου τὰ ἔργα καὶ τὴν θλῖψιν (I know your works and tribulation)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I know your <mark class="chg">works, and tribulation,</mark> and poverty (but you are rich), and the blasphemy of those who say they are Jews and are not, but are a synagogue of Satan.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I know your <mark class="chg">tribulation</mark> and poverty (but you are rich), and the blasphemy of those who say they are Jews and are not, but are a synagogue of Satan.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT Οἶδα τὰ ἔργα σου καὶ ποῦ κατοικεῖς (your works and where you dwell)" data-ref="Revelation 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:13</span><span class="srnote">RT Οἶδα τὰ ἔργα σου καὶ ποῦ κατοικεῖς (your works and where you dwell)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I know <mark class="chg">your works, and</mark> where you dwell, where Satan's throne is. <tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads των αγιων προφητων (God of the holy prophets); the CT reads των πνευματων των προφητων, so “of the spirits” must go and “holy” come in" data-ref="Revelation 22:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:6</span><span class="srnote">RT reads των αγιων προφητων (God of the holy prophets); the CT follows that reading's των πνευματων των προφητων, so “of the spirits” must go and “holy” come in</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then he said to me, "These words are faithful and true." <tr class="tr" data-note="RT δικαιωθήτω (’let him be righteous still’), not the critical δικαιοσύνην ποιησάτω" data-ref="Revelation 22:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:11</span><span class="srnote">RT δικαιωθήτω ('let him be righteous still'), not the critical δικαιοσύνην ποιησάτω</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He who is unjust, let him be unjust still; he who is filthy, let him be filthy still; he who is righteous, let him <mark class="chg">be righteous</mark> still; he who is holy, let him be holy still.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He who is unjust, let him be unjust still; he who is filthy, let him be filthy still; he who is righteous, let him <mark class="chg">practice righteousness</mark> still; he who is holy, let him be holy still.</span></span></tr>

RT also lists three separate losses: the book of life, the holy city, and what is written in this book</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If anyone takes away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part <mark class="chg">from</mark> the <mark class="chg">Book</mark> of <mark class="chg">Life, from</mark> the holy city, <mark class="chg">and from the things</mark> which are written in this book.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If anyone takes away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part <mark class="chg">in</mark> the <mark class="chg">tree</mark> of <mark class="chg">life and in</mark> the holy city, which are written in this book.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="RT reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου (our Lord Jesus Christ); the CT’s “the Lord Jesus” drops both ημων and Χριστου" data-ref="Revelation 22:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:21</span><span class="srnote">RT reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου (our Lord Jesus Christ); the CT’s “the Lord Jesus” drops both ημων and Χριστου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of <mark class="chg">our</mark> Lord Jesus <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> be with all. </tbody></table></div>

The nine places a critical text still brackets outright (Mark 16:9–20, John 7:53–8:11 and the rest) are a different question, so they are not in this table. Why any of this happened, and why I read an NKJV, is in [the essay](/nkjv/).
